---
sidebarDepth: 1
title: "V4.0.2升级到V4.0.3"
---
## 调用链系统升级

**_第一步_**：查找原部署安装目录
```
#获取进程id
jps -l | grep skywalking 
#获取部署目录
pwdx 进程id 
```
**_第二步_**：停止并备份原服务

```
#停止调用链服务
kill -9 进程id   

# 进入部署目录进行备份
mv apache-skywalking-apm-bin apache-skywalking-apm-bin.back
```
**_第三步_**：部署升级包
```
#解压 
tar -zxvf apache-skywalking-apm-bin.tar.gz
#复制备份的文件 application.yml 到当前目录, 选择覆盖此文件
cp apache-skywalking-apm-bin.back/config/application.yml apache-skywalking-apm-bin/config/application.yml
#修改application.yml文件
增加如下配置
auth:
  selector: default
  default:
#修改auth-settings.yml 中的ip、port 指向正确的auth服务
auth:
  ip: 127.0.0.1
  port: 9004
  #开关 默认开启
  switch: true
#在apache-skywalking-apm-bin/bin 目录下执行启动命令
sh oapService.sh 

```
**_第四步_**：访问验证
```
#访问管理端进行验证
http://ip/observer-center/skywalking/#/general?trace=true
```

## 日志服务系统升级

**_第一步_**：查找原部署安装目录

```
jps -l | grep st-logplatform-service.jar #获取进程id
pwdx 进程id #获取部署目录
```

**_第二步_**：停止并备份原服务

```
#在日志服务st-logplatform-service目录下执行
sh run.sh stop #停止日志服务

# 回到上层目录执行备份
mv st-logplatform-service st-logplatform-service.bak.20240314
```

**_第三步_**：部署升级包

```
#解压
unzip st-logplatform-service.zip

#复制备份的application.yml文件到当前目录, 选择覆盖此文件
cp st-logplatform-service.bak.20240314/config/application.yml st-logplatform-service/config/application.yml

#修改application.yml文件
增加如下配置, 修改为正确的auth服务地址
st-log-platform:
  auth-enable: true    #是否开启接口鉴权
  auth-server: 127.0.0.1:9004    # 鉴权服务ip和端口

#在st-logplatform-service目录下执行启动命令
sh run.sh start

# 查看是否成功启动
jps -l | grep st-logplatform-service.jar
```

**_第四步_**：访问验证

```
#访问管理端进行验证
http://ip/observer-center/observer-micro/#/log-analysis
```

## 用户权限管理系统升级

**_注意_**：

升级手册基于上版本 Mysql 数据库，如要切换数据库，可通过 Mysql 可视化工具导出 sql，并通过达梦迁移工具，导入 sql。

达梦数据库部署步骤见《星迹可观测中间件部署文档》

### 升级 resource-manager-service 组件

**_概述_**：停服并备份原服务，解压新包，修改配置，启动新包

**_注意_**：步骤命令中的文档路径仅作范例，具体以实际为准

**_第一步_**：查找服务目录

```
[root@iflyops-1 ~]# ps -ef | grep resource-manager-service-1.0.0-SNAPSHOT.jar | grep java | grep -v grep | awk '{print $2}'
3710
[root@iflyops-1 ~]# pwdx 3710
3710: /data/service/resource-manager-service
```

**_第二步_**：停服并备份

停服

```
[root@iflyops-1 ~]# cd /data/service/resource-manager-service
[root@iflyops-1 resource-manager-service]# ./run.sh stop
shut down resource-service server(3710)
```

检查进程已关闭

```
[root@iflyops-1 resource-manager-service]# ps -ef | grep resource-manager-service-1.0.0-SNAPSHOT.jar | grep java | grep -v grep
[root@iflyops-1 resource-manager-service]#
```

改名作备份

```
[root@iflyops-1 resource-manager-service]# cd /data/service
[root@iflyops-1 service]# mv resource-manager-service resource-manager-service.bak.20240228
```

**_第三步_**：部署升级包

将部署包里的`resource-manager-service.zip`上传到`/data/service/`下，解压后会生成`resource-manager-service`文件夹。

1、解压：

```
[root@iflyops-1 service]# cd /data/service/
[root@iflyops-1 service]# unzip resource-manager-service.zip
```

2、赋予启动脚本执行权限

```
[root@iflyops-1 service]# cd /data/service/resource-manager-service
[root@iflyops-1 resource-manager-service]# chmod 755 -R run.sh
```

3、修改配置文件 application-prod.yml

```
[root@iflyops-1 resource-manager-service]# cd /data/service/resource-manager-service/config
[root@iflyops-1 resource-manager-service]# vim application-prod.yml
```

这里需要修改四处

```yml
server:
  # 待改项1：服务端口，与原服务保持一致
  port: 9006
  servlet:
    context-path: /resource

spring:
  datasource:
    type: com.alibaba.druid.pool.DruidDataSource
    druid:
      # 待改项2：数据库连接信息，需修改IP、端口、账号、密码，与原服务保持一致。达梦数据库则配置达梦，注释Mysql连接；Mysql数据库则配置Mysql，注释达梦连接
      ######### DM数据库配置 start #########
      #      driver-class-name: dm.jdbc.driver.DmDriver
      #      url: jdbc:dm://{dameng-ip}:5236/AIOPS_RESOURCE?useUnicode=true&characterEncoding=UTF-8&serverTimezone=Asia/Shanghai
      #      username: SYSDBA
      #      password: SYSDBA
      ######### DM数据库配置 end #########
      ######### Mysql数据库配置 start #########
      driver-class-name: com.mysql.cj.jdbc.Driver
      url: jdbc:mysql://{mysql-ip}:3306/aiops_resource?useUnicode=true&characterEncoding=UTF-8&serverTimezone=Asia/Shanghai
      username: root
      password: 123456
      ######### Mysql数据库配置 end #########
      ### 此处对应配置文件中druid默认配置，此部署文档中就不显示了

## 待改项3：权限门户系统服务地址，需修改IP、端口，与原服务保持一致
auth-url: http://{auth-server-ip}:9004/auth

## 待改项4：超管账号，与原服务保持一致。
init-admin-account: admin
```

4、启动 resource-manager-service

```
[root@iflyops-1 resource-manager-service]# cd /data/service/resource-manager-service/
[root@iflyops-1 resource-manager-service]# ./run.sh start
```

5、验证服务进程启动成功

```
[root@iflyops-1 resource-manager-service]# ps -ef | grep resource-manager-service-1.0.0-SNAPSHOT.jar | grep java | grep -v grep
```

### 失败回滚

1、检查服务进程，关闭后删除升级包，并还原备份包文件夹名称，再启动

## 日志 sdk 升级

### 升级说明

1. 将服务引入的日志 sdk 版本的依赖替换成 1.5-general

```
  <dependency>
            <groupId>com.iflytek.itsc.apm</groupId>
            <artifactId>apm-toolkit-logback-1.x</artifactId>
            <version>1.5-SNAPSHOT</version>
        </dependency>

        <dependency>
            <groupId>com.iflytek.itsc.apm</groupId>
            <artifactId>apm-toolkit-log4j-2.x</artifactId>
            <version>1.5-SNAPSHOT</version>
        </dependency>


<dependency>
            <groupId>com.iflytek.itsc.apm</groupId>
            <artifactId>apm-toolkit-log4j-1.x</artifactId>
            <version>1.5-SNAPSHOT</version>
        </dependency>
```

2. 重新打包业务服务
3. 将原有服务部署备份和停止进程
4. 将服务重新启动

### 验证

业务日志正常收集

### 失败回滚

1. 修改依赖版本号到上一个版本
2. 重新打包部署

## 权限门户升级

### 升级说明

1. 将服务器上原有的 auth-service 下的 auth-service-1.0.0-SNAPSHOT.jar 备份

```
cp auth-service-1.0.0-SNAPSHOT.jar auth-service-1.0.0-SNAPSHOT.jar.bak
```

2. 停止 auth 进程

```shell
cd ./auth-service
sh run.sh stop
```

3. 将部署包中的 auth-service.zip 解压后获取到 auth-service-1.0.0-SNAPSHOT.jar 上传到服务器

4. 将服务重新启动

```shell
cd ./auth-service
sh run.sh start
```

### 验证

1. auth-service 服务正常启动

```bash
ps -ef | grep auth-service
```

可以查看到相关进程，说明部署成功

### 失败回滚

1、检查服务进程，关闭后删除升级包，并还原备份包，再启动

## 前端升级

### 升级说明

1.将原有的 micro_front_observer 进行备份

2.进入 nginx 目录下，将新的前端部署包里的 micro_front_observer.zip 上传并解压

3.进入/nginx/micro_front_observer/iflytek-observer 目录下，打开 config.js 文件，将 production 对象里 127.0.0.1 改为当前部署机器的 ip 或域名；

```
"production":{
"VITE_SKYWALKING" : "http://127.0.0.1/skywalking/", #改为当前机器的服务 ip 或域名
"VITE_AOPS_MONITOR" : "http://127.0.0.1/aops-monitor/", #改为当前机器的服务 ip或域名
"VITE_BASIC_AGENT" :"http://127.0.0.1/basic-agent/", #改为当前机器的服务 ip 或域名
"VITE_OBSERVER_MICRO":"http://127.0.0.1/observer-micro/", #改为当前机器的服务ip 或域名
"VITE_DOC_URL" :"http://127.0.0.1/sop_doc/" #改为当前机器的服务 ip 或域名
"VITE_LOGIN_CONFIG": {
	"mode": "normal", // "normal": 本地登陆 / "sso": sso 登陆 / "uap": uap 登陆
	"sso_url": "https://ssotest.iflytek.com/sso",
	"uap_url": "http://127.0.0.1:8380/uap-server",
	"app_code": "yy_1704868151449",
	"client_platform": "myuap"
	}
}
```

### 验证

访问项目查看是否正常运行

### 失败回滚

删除升级包，还原备份包，重启 nginx，访问前端页面

## gse 升级

### 升级说明

1. 将服务器上原有的 gse 目录下的 st_gse 备份

```shell
cp st_gse st_gse_20240315bak
```

2. 停止 gse 进程

```shell
sh stop.sh
```

3. 将部署包中的 gse_service.zip 解压后获取到 st_gse 上传到服务器

4. 将服务重新启动

```shell
sh start.sh
```

### 验证

1. 查看 gse 服务是否正常启动

```bash
ps -ef | grep st_gse
```

可以查看到相关进程，日志无异常输出，说明部署成功

### 失败回滚

1、查看日志内容，记录失败原因；
2、检查服务进程，关闭后删除升级包，并还原备份包，再启动

## n9e 升级

### 升级说明

1. 将数据库n9e_v6的表integrate备份

2. 执行sql

```shell

    "metrics": ""

    "instruction": "### 前置条件\\n\\n- ElasticSearch 版本 >= 6.0.0\\n\\n- ElasticSearch 默认采集 `Node Stats` 指标，如果需要采集 `Cluster-Health` 相关指标，需要设置 `cluster_health = true`\\n\\n- 设置\\n\\n   \\n\\n  ```\\n  cluster_health = true\\n  ```\\n\\n   \\n\\n  可产生如下指标集\\n\\n  - `elasticsearch_cluster_health`\\n\\n- 设置\\n\\n   \\n\\n  ```\\n  cluster_stats = true\\n  ```\\n\\n   \\n\\n  可产生如下指标集\\n\\n  - :----------------------------------------------------------- |\\n| `cluster_name` | Name of the cluster, based on the Cluster name setting setting. |\\n| `index_name`   | Name of the index. The name \'_all\' target all data streams and indices in a cluster. |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric                                | Description                                                  | Type  | Unit  |\\n| :------------------------------------ | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ----- |\\n| `total_flush_total`                   | Number of flush operations.                                  | float | count |\\n| `total_flush_total_time_in_millis`    | Total time in milliseconds spent performing flush operations. | float | ms    |\\n| `total_get_missing_total`             | Total number of failed get operations.                       | float | count |\\n| `total_indexing_index_current`        | Number of indexing operations currently running.             | float | count |\\n| `total_indexing_index_time_in_millis` | Total time in milliseconds spent performing indexing operations. | float | ms    |\\n| `total_indexing_index_total`          | Total number of indexing operations.                         | float | count |\\n| `total_merges_current_docs`           | Number of document merges currently running.                 | float | count |\\n| `total_merges_total`                  | Total number of merge operations.                            | float | count |\\n| `total_merges_total_docs`             | Total number of merged documents.                            | float | count |\\n| `total_merges_total_time_in_millis`   | Total time in milliseconds spent performing merge operations. | float | ms    |\\n| `total_refresh_total`                 | Total number of refresh operations.                          | float | count |\\n| `total_refresh_total_time_in_millis`  | Total time in milliseconds spent performing refresh operations. | float | ms    |\\n| `total_search_fetch_current`          | Number of fetch operations currently running.                | float | count |\\n| `total_search_fetch_time_in_millis`   | Time in milliseconds spent performing fetch operations.      | float | ms    |\\n| `total_search_fetch_total`            | Total number of fetch operations.                            | float | count |\\n| `total_search_query_current`          | Number of query operations currently running.                | float | count |\\n| `total_search_query_time_in_millis`   | Time in milliseconds spent performing query operations.      | float | ms    |\\n| `total_search_query_total`            | Total number of query operations.                            | float | count |\\n| `total_store_size_in_bytes`           | Total size, in bytes, of all shards assigned to selected nodes. | float | B     |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `elasticsearch_cluster_stats`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag            | Description                                                  |\\n| :------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- |\\n| `cluster_name` | Name of the cluster, based on the cluster.name setting.      |\\n| `node_name`    | Name of the node.                                            |\\n| `status`       | Health status of the cluster, based on the state of its primary and replica shards. |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric                                    | Description                                                  | Type  | Unit  |\\n| :---------------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ----- |\\n| `nodes_process_open_file_descriptors_avg` | Average number of concurrently open file descriptors. Returns -1 if not supported. | float | count |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `elasticsearch_cluster_health`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag              | Description                             |\\n| :--------------- | :-------------------------------------- |\\n| `cluster_name`   | Name of the cluster.                    |\\n| `cluster_status` | The cluster status: red, yellow, green. |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric                          | Description                                                  | Type | Unit  |\\n| :------------------------------ | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ---- | ----- |\\n| `active_primary_shards`         | The number of active primary shards in the cluster.          | int  | count |\\n| `active_shards`                 | The number of active shards in the cluster.                  | int  | count |\\n| `indices_lifecycle_error_count` | The number of indices that are managed by ILM and are in an error state. | int  | count |\\n| `initializing_shards`           | The number of shards that are currently initializing.        | int  | count |\\n| `number_of_data_nodes`          | The number of data nodes in the cluster.                     | int  | count |\\n| `number_of_pending_tasks`       | The total number of pending tasks.                           | int  | count |\\n| `relocating_shards`             | The number of shards that are relocating from one node to another. | int  | count |\\n| `status_code`                   | The health as a number: red = 3, yellow = 2, green = 1.      | int  | count |\\n| `unassigned_shards`             | The number of shards that are unassigned to a node.          | int  | count |"}');
INSERT INTO integrate (id, name, `key`, cate, path, display) VALUES (3, 'Influxdb', 'influxdb', 1, 'influxdb/influxdb.conf', '{
    "instruction": "### 前置条件\\n\\n- InfluxDB 采集器，仅适用于 InfluxDB v1.x\\n- InfluxDB v2.x ，需要使用 prom 采集器进行采集\\n\\n已测试的版本：\\n\\n- 1.8.10",
    "metrics": "### `influxdb_cq`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag | Description |\\n| :----- | :--------------- |\\n| `host` | System hostname. |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric | Description | Type | Unit |\\n| :----------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ---- |\\n| `query_fail` | The total number of continuous queries that executed but failed. | float | - |\\n| `query_ok` | The total number of continuous queries that executed successfully. | float | - |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `influxdb_database`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag | Description |\\n| :--------- | :--------------- |\\n| `database` | Database name. |\\n| `host` | System hostname. |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric | Description | Type | Unit |\\n| :----------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ---- |\\n| `num_measurements` | The current number of measurements in the specified database. | float | - |\\n| `num_series` | The current series cardinality of the specified database. | float | - |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `influxdb_httpd`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag | Description |\\n| :----- | :--------------- |\\n| `bind` | Bind port. |\\n| `host` | System hostname. |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric | Description | Type | Unit |\\n| :--------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ---- |\\n| `auth_fail` | The number of HTTP requests that were aborted due to authentication being required, but not supplied or incorrect. | float | - |\\n| `client_error` | The number of HTTP responses due to client errors, with a 4XX HTTP status code. | float | - |\\n| `flux_query_req` | The number of Flux query requests served. | float | - |\\n| `flux_query_req_duration_ns` | The duration (wall-time), in nanoseconds, spent executing Flux query requests. | float | ns |\\n| `ping_req` | The number of times InfluxDB HTTP server served the /ping HTTP endpoint. | float | - |\\n| `points_written_dropped` | The number of points dropped by the storage engine. | float | - |\\n| `points_written_fail` | The number of points accepted by the HTTP /write endpoint, but unable to be persisted. | float | - |\\n| `points_written_ok` | The number of points successfully accepted and persisted by the HTTP /write endpoint. | float | - |\\n| `prom_read_req` | The number of read requests to the Prometheus /read endpoint. | float | - |\\n| `prom_write_req` | The number of write requests to the Prometheus /write endpoint. | float | - |\\n| `query_req` | The number of query requests. | float | - |\\n| `query_req_duration_ns` | The total query request duration, in nanosecond (ns). | float | ns |\\n| `query_resp_bytes` | The total number of bytes returned in query responses. | float | B |\\n| `recovered_panics` | The total number of panics recovered by the HTTP handler. | float | - |\\n| `req` | The total number of HTTP requests served. | float | - |\\n| `req_active` | The number of currently active requests. | float | - |\\n| `req_duration_ns` | The duration (wall time), in nanoseconds, spent inside HTTP requests. | float | ns |\\n| `server_error` | The number of HTTP responses due to server errors. | float | - |\\n| `status_req` | The number of status requests served using the HTTP /status endpoint. | float | - |\\n| `values_written_ok` | The number of values (fields) successfully accepted and persisted by the HTTP /write endpoint. | float | - |\\n| `write_req` | The number of write requests served using the HTTP /write endpoint. | float | - |\\n| `write_req_active` | The number of currently active write requests. | float | - |\\n| `write_req_bytes` | The total number of bytes of line protocol data received by write requests, using the HTTP /write endpoint. | float | B |\\n| `write_req_duration_ns` | The duration (wall time), in nanoseconds, of write requests served using the /write HTTP endpoint. | float | ns |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `influxdb_memstats`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag | Description |\\n| :----- | :--------------- |\\n| `host` | System hostname. |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric | Description | Type | Unit |\\n| :---------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ---- |\\n| `alloc` | The currently allocated number of bytes of heap objects. | float | B |\\n| `buck_hash_sys` | The bytes of memory in profiling bucket hash tables. | float | B |\\n| `frees` | The cumulative number of freed (live) heap objects. | float | - |\\n| `gc_cpu_fraction` | The fraction of CPU time used by the garbage collection cycle. | float | - |\\n| `gc_sys` | The bytes of memory in garbage collection metadata. | float | B |\\n| `heap_alloc` | The size, in bytes, of all heap objects. | float | B |\\n| `heap_idle` | The number of bytes of idle heap objects. | float | B |\\n| `heap_inuse` | The number of bytes in in-use spans. | float | B |\\n| `heap_objects` | The number of allocated heap objects. | float | - |\\n| `heap_released` | The number of bytes of physical memory returned to the OS. | float | B |\\n| `heap_sys` | The number of bytes of heap memory obtained from the OS. | float | B |\\n| `last_gc` | Time the last garbage collection finished, as nanoseconds since 1970 (the UNIX epoch). | float | nsec |\\n| `lookups` | The number of pointer lookups performed by the runtime. | float | - |\\n| `mallocs` | The total number of heap objects allocated. | float | - |\\n| `mcache_inuse` | The bytes of allocated mcache structures. | float | B |\\n| `mcache_sys` | The bytes of memory obtained from the OS for mcache structures. | float | B |\\n| `mspan_inuse` | The bytes of allocated mcache structures. | float | B |\\n| `mspan_sys` | The bytes of memory obtained from the OS for `mspan`. | float | B |\\n| `next_gc` | The target heap size of the next garbage collection cycle. | float | - |\\n| `num_forced_gc` | The number of GC cycles that were forced by the application calling the GC function. | float | - |\\n| `num_gc` | The number of completed garbage collection cycles. | float | - |\\n| `other_sys` | The number of bytes of memory used other than `heap_sys/stacks_sys/mspan_sys/mcache_sys/buckhash_sys/gc_sys`. | float | B |\\n| `pause_ns` | The time garbage collection cycles are paused in nanoseconds. | float | ns |\\n| `pause_total_ns` | The total time garbage collection cycles are paused in nanoseconds. | float | ns |\\n| `stack_inuse` | The number of bytes in in-use stacks. | float | B |\\n| `stack_sys` | The total number of bytes of memory obtained from the stack in use. | float | B |\\n| `sys` | The cumulative bytes allocated for heap objects. | float | B |\\n| `total_alloc` | The cumulative bytes allocated for heap objects. | float | B |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `influxdb_queryExecutor`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag | Description |\\n| :----- | :--------------- |\\n| `host` | System hostname. |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric | Description | Type | Unit |\\n| :------------------ | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ---- |\\n| `queries_active` | The number of active queries currently being handled. | float | - |\\n| `queries_executed` | The number of queries executed (started). | float | - |\\n| `queries_finished` | The number of queries that have finished executing. | float | - |\\n| `query_duration_ns` | The duration (wall time), in nanoseconds, of every query executed. | float | ns |\\n| `recovered_panics` | The number of panics recovered by the Query Executor. | float | - |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `influxdb_runtime`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag | Description |\\n| :----- | :--------------- |\\n| `host` | System hostname. |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric | Description | Type | Unit |\\n| :--------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ---- |\\n| `alloc` | The currently allocated number of bytes of heap objects. | float | B |\\n| `frees` | The cumulative number of freed (live) heap objects. | float | - |\\n| `heap_alloc` | The size, in bytes, of all heap objects. | float | B |\\n| `heap_idle` | The number of bytes of idle heap objects. | float | B |\\n| `heap_inuse` | The number of bytes in in-use spans. | float | B |\\n| `heap_objects` | The number of allocated heap objects. | float | - |\\n| `heap_released` | The number of bytes of physical memory returned to the OS. | float | B |\\n| `heap_sys` | The number of bytes of heap memory obtained from the OS. | float | B |\\n| `lookups` | The number of pointer lookups performed by the runtime. | float | - |\\n| `mallocs` | The total number of heap objects allocated. | float | - |\\n| `num_gc` | The number of completed garbage collection cycles. | float | - |\\n| `num_goroutine` | The total number of Go routines. | float | - |\\n| `pause_total_ns` | The total time garbage collection cycles are paused in nanoseconds. | float | ns |\\n| `sys` | The cumulative bytes allocated for heap objects. | float | B |\\n| `total_alloc` | The cumulative bytes allocated for heap objects. | float | B |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `influxdb_shard`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag | Description |\\n| :----------------- | :---------------- |\\n| `database` | Database name. |\\n| `engine` | Engine. |\\n| `host` | System hostname. |\\n| `id` | ID. |\\n| `index_type` | Index type. |\\n| `path` | Path. |\\n| `retention_policy` | Retention policy. |\\n| `wal_path` | Wal path. |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric | Description | Type | Unit |\\n| :--------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ---- |\\n| `disk_bytes` | The size, in bytes, of the shard, including the size of the data directory and the WAL directory. | float | B |\\n| `fields_create` | The number of fields created. | float | - |\\n| `series_create` | Then number of series created. | float | - |\\n| `write_bytes` | The number of bytes written to the shard. | float | B |\\n| `write_points_dropped` | The number of requests to write points t dropped from a write. | float | - |\\n| `write_points_err` | The number of requests to write points that failed to be written due to errors. | float | - |\\n| `write_points_ok` | The number of points written successfully. | float | - |\\n| `write_req` | The total number of write requests. | float | - |\\n| `write_req_err` | The total number of write requests that failed due to errors. | float | - |\\n| `write_req_ok` | The total number of successful write requests. | float | - |\\n| `write_values_ok` | The number of write values successfully. | float | - |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `influxdb_subscriber`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag | Description |\\n| :----- | :--------------- |\\n| `host` | System hostname. |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric | Description | Type | Unit |\\n| :---------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ---- |\\n| `create_failures` | The number of subscriptions that failed to be created. | float | - |\\n| `points_written` | The total number of points that were successfully written to subscribers. | float | - |\\n| `write_failures` | The total number of batches that failed to be written to subscribers. | float | - |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `influxdb_tsm1_cache`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag | Description |\\n| :----------------- | :---------------- |\\n| `database` | Database name. |\\n| `engine` | Engine. |\\n| `host` | System hostname. |\\n| `id` | ID. |\\n| `index_type` | Index type. |\\n| `path` | Path. |\\n| `retention_policy` | Retention policy. |\\n| `wal_path` | Wal path. |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric | Description | Type | Unit |\\n| :----------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ---- |\\n| `cache_age_ms` | The duration, in milliseconds, since the cache was last snapshotted at sample time. | float | ms |\\n| `cached_bytes` | The total number of bytes that have been written into snapshots. | float | B |\\n| `disk_bytes` | The size, in bytes, of on-disk snapshots. | float | B |\\n| `mem_bytes` | The size, in bytes, of in-memory cache. | float | B |\\n| `snapshot_count` | The current level (number) of active snapshots. | float | - |\\n| `wal_compaction_time_ms` | The duration, in milliseconds, that the commit lock is held while compacting snapshots. | float | ms |\\n| `write_dropped` | The total number of writes dropped due to timeouts. | float | - |\\n| `write_err` | The total number of writes that failed. | float | - |\\n| `write_ok` | The total number of successful writes. | float | - |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `influxdb_tsm1_engine`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag | Description |\\n| :----------------- | :---------------- |\\n| `database` | Database name. |\\n| `engine` | Engine. |\\n| `host` | System hostname. |\\n| `id` | ID. |\\n| `index_type` | Index type. |\\n| `path` | Path. |\\n| `retention_policy` | Retention policy. |\\n| `wal_path` | Wal path. |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric | Description | Type | Unit |\\n| :--------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ---- |\\n| `cache_compaction_duration` | The duration (wall time), in nanoseconds, spent in cache compactions. | float | ns |\\n| `cache_compaction_err` | The number of cache compactions that have failed due to errors. | float | - |\\n| `cache_compactions` | The total number of cache compactions that have ever run. | float | - |\\n| `cache_compactions_active` | The number of cache compactions that are currently running. | float | - |\\n| `tsm_full_compaction_duration` | The duration (wall time), in nanoseconds, spent in full compactions. | float | - |\\n| `tsm_full_compaction_err` | The total number of TSM full compactions that have failed due to errors. | float | - |\\n| `tsm_full_compaction_queue` | The current number of pending TMS Full compactions. | float | - |\\n| `tsm_full_compactions` | The total number of TSM full compactions that have ever run. | float | - |\\n| `tsm_full_compactions_active` | The number of TSM full compactions currently running. | float | - |\\n| `tsm_level1_compaction_duration` | The duration (wall time), in nanoseconds, spent in TSM level 1 compactions. | float | ns |\\n| `tsm_level1_compaction_err` | The total number of TSM level 1 compactions that have failed due to errors. | float | - |\\n| `tsm_level1_compaction_queue` | The current number of pending TSM level 1 compactions. | float | - |\\n| `tsm_level1_compactions` | The total number of TSM level 1 compactions that have ever run. | float | - |\\n| `tsm_level1_compactions_active` | The number of TSM level 1 compactions that are currently running. | float | - |\\n| `tsm_level2_compaction_duration` | The duration (wall time), in nanoseconds, spent in TSM level 2 compactions. | float | ns |\\n| `tsm_level2_compaction_err` | The number of TSM level 2 compactions that have failed due to errors. | float | - |\\n| `tsm_level2_compaction_queue` | The current number of pending TSM level 2 compactions. | float | - |\\n| `tsm_level2_compactions` | The total number of TSM level 2 compactions that have ever run. | float | - |\\n| `tsm_level2_compactions_active` | The number of TSM level 2 compactions that are currently running. | float | - |\\n| `tsm_level3_compaction_duration` | The duration (wall time), in nanoseconds, spent in TSM level 3 compactions. | float | ns |\\n| `tsm_level3_compaction_err` | The number of TSM level 3 compactions that have failed due to errors. | float | - |\\n| `tsm_level3_compaction_queue` | The current number of pending TSM level 3 compactions. | float | - |\\n| `tsm_level3_compactions` | The total number of TSM level 3 compactions that have ever run. | float | - |\\n| `tsm_level3_compactions_active` | The number of TSM level 3 compactions that are currently running. | float | - |\\n| `tsm_optimize_compaction_duration` | The duration (wall time), in nanoseconds, spent during TSM optimize compactions. | float | ns |\\n| `tsm_optimize_compaction_err` | The total number of TSM optimize compactions that have failed due to errors. | float | - |\\n| `tsm_optimize_compaction_queue` | The current number of pending TSM optimize compactions. | float | - |\\n| `tsm_optimize_compactions` | The total number of TSM optimize compactions that have ever run. | float | - |\\n| `tsm_optimize_compactions_active` | The number of TSM optimize compactions that are currently running. | float | - |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `influxdb_tsm1_filestore`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag | Description |\\n| :----------------- | :---------------- |\\n| `database` | Database name. |\\n| `engine` | Engine. |\\n| `host` | System hostname. |\\n| `id` | ID. |\\n| `index_type` | Index type. |\\n| `path` | Path. |\\n| `retention_policy` | Retention policy. |\\n| `wal_path` | Wal path. |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric | Description | Type | Unit |\\n| :----------- | :------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ---- |\\n| `disk_bytes` | The size, in bytes, of disk usage by the TSM file store. | float | B |\\n| `num_files` | The total number of files in the TSM file store. | float | - |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `influxdb_tsm1_wal`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag | Description |\\n| :----------------- | :---------------- |\\n| `database` | Database name. |\\n| `engine` | Engine. |\\n| `host` | System hostname. |\\n| `id` | ID. |\\n| `index_type` | Index type. |\\n| `path` | Path. |\\n| `retention_policy` | Retention policy. |\\n| `wal_path` | Wal path. |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric | Description | Type | Unit |\\n| :--------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------- | ----- | ---- |\\n| `current_segment_disk_bytes` | The current size, in bytes, of the segment disk. | float | B |\\n| `old_segments_disk_bytes` | The size, in bytes, of the segment disk. | float | B |\\n| `write_err` | The number of writes that failed due to errors. | float | - |\\n| `write_ok` | The number of writes that succeeded. | float | - |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `influxdb_write`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag | Description |\\n| :----- | :--------------- |\\n| `host` | System hostname. |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric | Description | Type | Unit |\\n| :---------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ---- |\\n| `point_req` | The total number of every point requested to be written to this data node. | float | - |\\n| `point_req_local` | The total number of point requests that have been attempted to be written into a shard on the same (local) node. | float | - |\\n| `req` | The total number of batches of points requested to be written to this node. | float | - |\\n| `sub_write_drop` | The total number of batches of points that failed to be sent to the subscription dispatcher. | float | - |\\n| `sub_write_ok` | The total number of batches of points that were successfully sent to the subscription dispatcher. | float | - |\\n| `write_drop` | The total number of write requests for points that have been dropped due to timestamps not matching any existing retention policies. | float | - |\\n| `write_error` | The total number of batches of points that were not successfully written, due to a failure to write to a local or remote shard. | float | - |\\n| `write_ok` | The total number of batches of points written at the requested consistency level. | float | - |\\n| `write_timeout` | The total number of write requests that failed to complete within the default write timeout duration. | float | - |"
}');
INSERT INTO integrate (id, name, `key`, cate, path, display) VALUES (4, 'Mongodb', 'mongodb', 1, 'db/mongodb.conf', '{
    "instruction": "### 前置条件\\n\\n- 已测试的版本：\\n  - 6.0\\n  - 5.0\\n  - 4.0\\n  - 3.0\\n- 开发使用 MongoDB 版本 `4.4.5`;\\n- 编写配置文件在对应目录下然后启动 datasleuth 即可完成配置；\\n- 使用 TLS 进行安全连接请在配置文件中配置 `## TLS connection config` 下响应证书文件路径与配置；\\n- 如果 MongoDb 启动了访问控制那么需要配置必须的用户权限用于建立授权连接。例如：\\n\\n```\\n> db.grantRolesToUser(\\"user\\", [{role: \\"read\\", actions: \\"find\\", db: \\"local\\"}])\\n```\\n",
    "metrics": "- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric                                      | Description                                                  | Type  | Unit  |\\n| :------------------------------------------ | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ----- |\\n| `active_reads`                              | The number of the active client connections performing read operations. | int   | count |\\n| `active_writes`                             | The number of active client connections performing write operations. | int   | count |\\n| `aggregate_command_failed`                  | The number of times that \'aggregate\' command failed on this mongod | int   | count |\\n| `aggregate_command_total`                   | The number of times that \'aggregate\' command executed on this mongod. | int   | count |\\n| `assert_msg`                                | The number of message assertions raised since the MongoDB process started. Check the log file for more information about these messages. | int   | count |\\n| `assert_regular`                            | The number of regular assertions raised since the MongoDB process started. Check the log file for more information about these messages. | int   | count |\\n| `assert_rollovers`                          | The number of times that the rollover counters have rolled over since the last time the MongoDB process started. The counters will rollover to zero after 2 30 assertions. Use this value to provide context to the other values in the asserts data structure. | int   | count |\\n| `assert_user`                               | The number of \\"user asserts\\" that have occurred since the last time the MongoDB process started. These are errors that user may generate, such as out of disk space or duplicate key. You can prevent these assertions by fixing a problem with your application or deployment. Check the MongoDB log for more information. | int   | count |\\n| `assert_warning`                            | Changed in version 4.0. Starting in MongoDB 4.0, the field returns zero 0.       | (Existed in 3.0 and earlier version)                         | int  | count |\\n| `wtcache_bytes_written_from`           | (Existed in 3.0 and earlier version)                         | int  | count |\\n| `wtcache_current_bytes`                | (Existed in 3.0 and earlier version)                         | int  | count |\\n| `wtcache_internal_pages_evicted`       | (Existed in 3.0 and earlier version)                         | int  | count |\\n| `wtcache_max_bytes_configured`         | Maximum cache size. (Existed in 3.0 and earlier version)     | int  | count |\\n| `wtcache_modified_pages_evicted`       | (Existed in 3.0 and earlier version)                         | int  | count |\\n| `wtcache_pages_evicted_by_app_thread`  | (Existed in 3.0 and earlier version)                         | int  | count |\\n| `wtcache_pages_queued_for_eviction`    | (Existed in 3.0 and earlier version)                         | int  | count |\\n| `wtcache_pages_read_into`              | Number of pages read into the cache. (Existed in 3.0 and earlier version) | int  | count |\\n| `wtcache_pages_requested_from`         | Number of pages request from the cache. (Existed in 3.0 and earlier version) | int  | count |\\n| `wtcache_pages_written_from`           | Pages written from cache. (Existed in 3.0 and earlier version) | int  | count |\\n| `wtcache_server_evicting_pages`        | (Existed in 3.0 and earlier version)                         | int  | count |\\n| `wtcache_tracked_dirty_bytes`          | (Existed in 3.0 and earlier version)                         | int  | count |\\n| `wtcache_unmodified_pages_evicted`     | Main statistics for page eviction. (Existed in 3.0 and earlier version) | int  | count |\\n| `wtcache_worker_thread_evictingpages`  | (Existed in 3.0 and earlier version)                         | int  | count |"}');
INSERT INTO integrate (id, name, `key`, cate, path, display) VALUES (5, 'Mysql', 'mysql', 1, 'db/mysql.conf', '{
    "instruction": "### 前置条件\\n\\n- MySQL 版本 5.7+\\n- 创建监控账号（若直接使用root账号作为监控账号，可以直接忽略此步骤。一般情况，需用 MySQL `root` 账号登陆才能创建 MySQL 用户）\\n\\n```\\nCREATE USER \'datasleuth\'@\'localhost\' IDENTIFIED BY \'<UNIQUEPASSWORD>\';\\n\\n-- MySQL 8.0+ create the datasleuth user with the native password hashing method\\nCREATE USER \'datasleuth\'@\'localhost\' IDENTIFIED WITH mysql_native_password by \'<UNIQUEPASSWORD>\';\\n```\\n\\n- 授权\\n\\n```\\nGRANT PROCESS ON *.* TO \'datasleuth\'@\'localhost\';\\nGRANT SELECT ON *.* TO \'datasleuth\'@\'localhost\';\\nshow databases like \'performance_schema\';\\nGRANT SELECT ON performance_schema.* TO \'datasleuth\'@\'localhost\';\\nGRANT SELECT ON mysql.user TO \'datasleuth\'@\'localhost\';\\nGRANT replication client on *.*  to \'datasleuth\'@\'localhost\';\\n```\\n\\n> 如用 localhost 时发现采集器有如下报错，需要将上述步骤的 localhost 换成 ::1Error 1045: Access denied for user \'datasleuth\'@\'localhost\' (using password: YES)以上创建、授权操作，均限定了 datasleuth 这个用户，只\\n>\\n> 能在 MySQL 主机上（localhost）访问 MySQL。如果需要对 MySQL 进行远程采集，建议将 localhost 替换成 %（表示 datasleuth 可以在任意机器上访问 MySQL），也可用特定的 datasleuth 安装机器地址。\\n\\n### Binlog 开启\\n\\n默认情况下，MySQL Binlog 是不开启的。如果要统计 Binlog 大小，需要开启 MySQL 对应 Binlog 功能：\\n\\n```\\n-- ON: 开启/OFF: 关闭\\nSHOW VARIABLES LIKE \'log_bin\';\\n```",
    "metrics": "- 字段列表\\n\\n| Metric                       | Description                                                  | Type | Unit    |\\n| :--------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ---- | ------- |\\n| `Aborted_clients`            | The number of connections that were aborted because the client died without closing the connection properly. | int  | count   |\\n| `Aborted_connects`           | The number of failed attempts to connect to the MySQL server. | int  | count   |\\n| `Binlog_cache_disk_use`      | The number of transactions that used the temporary binary log cache but that exceeded the value of binlog_cache_size and used a temporary file to store statements from the transaction. | int  | B       |\\n| `Binlog_cache_use`           | The number of transactions that used the binary log cache.   | int  | B       |\\n| `Binlog_space_usage_bytes`   | Total binary log file size.                                  | int  | B       |\\n| `Bytes_received`             | The number of bytes received from all clients.               | int  | B       |\\n| `Bytes_sent`                 | The number of bytes sent to all clients.                     | int  | B       |\\n| `Com_commit`                 | The number of times of commit statement has been executed.   | int  | count   |\\n| `Com_delete`                 | The number of times of delete statement has been executed.   | int  | count   |\\n| `Com_delete_multi`           | The number of times of delete-multi statement has been executed. | int  | count   |\\n| `Com_insert`                 | The number of times of insert statement has been executed.   | int  | count   |\\n| `Com_insert_select`          | The number of times of insert-select statement has been executed. | int  | count   |\\n| `Com_load`                   | The number of times of load statement has been executed.     | int  | count   |\\n| `Com_replace`                | The number of times of replace statement has been executed.  | int  | count   |\\n| `Com_replace_select`         | The number of times of replace-select statement has been executed. | int  | count   |\\n| `Com_rollback`               | The number of times of rollback statement has been executed. | int  | count   |\\n| `Com_select`                 | The number of times of select statement has been executed.   | int  | count   |\\n| `Com_update`                 | The number of times of update statement has been executed.   | int  | count   |\\n| `Com_update_multi`           | The number of times of update-mult has been executed.        | int  | count   |\\n| `Connections`                | The rate of connections to the server.                       | int  | count   |\\n| `Created_tmp_disk_tables`    | The rate of internal on-disk temporary tables created by second by the server while executing statements. | int  | count   |\\n| `Created_tmp_files`          | The rate of temporary files created by second.               | int  | count   |\\n| `Created_tmp_tables`         | The rate of internal temporary tables created by second by the server while executing statements. | int  | count   |\\n| `Handler_commit`             | The number of internal COMMIT statements.                    | int  | count   |\\n| `Handler_delete`             | The number of internal DELETE statements.                    | int  | count   |\\n| `Handler_prepare`            | The number of internal PREPARE statements.                   | int  | count   |\\n| `Handler_read_first`         | The number of internal READ_FIRST statements.                | int  | count   |\\n| `Handler_read_key`           | The number of internal READ_KEY statements.                  | int  | count   |\\n| `Handler_read_next`          | The number of internal READ_NEXT statements.                 | int  | count   |\\n| `Handler_read_prev`          | The number of internal READ_PREV statements.                 | int  | count   |\\n| `Handler_read_rnd`           | The number of internal READ_RND statements.                  | int  | count   |\\n| `Handler_read_rnd_next`      | The number of internal READ_RND_NEXT statements.             | int  | count   |\\n| `Handler_rollback`           | The number of internal ROLLBACK statements.                  | int  | count   |\\n| `Handler_update`             | The number of internal UPDATE statements.                    | int  | count   |\\n| `Handler_write`              | The number of internal WRITE statements.                     | int  | count   |\\n| `Key_buffer_bytes_unflushed` | MyISAM key buffer bytes unflushed.                           | int  | count   |\\n| `Key_buffer_bytes_used`      | MyISAM key buffer bytes used.                                | int  | count   |\\n| `Key_buffer_size`            | Size of the buffer used for index blocks.                    | int  | B       |\\n| `Key_cache_utilization`      | The key cache utilization ratio.                             | int  | percent |\\n| `Key_read_requests`          | The number of requests to read a key block from the MyISAM key cache. | int  | count   |\\n| `Key_reads`                  | The number of physical reads of a key block from disk into the MyISAM key cache. If Key_reads is large, then your key_buffer_size value is probably too small. The cache miss rate can be calculated as Key_reads/Key_read_requests. | int  | count   |\\n| `Key_write_requests`         | The number of requests to write a key block to the MyISAM key cache. | int  | count   |\\n| `Key_writes`                 | The number of physical writes of a key block from the MyISAM key cache to disk. | int  | count   |\\n| `Max_used_connections`       | The maximum number of connections that have been in use simultaneously since the server started. | int  | count   |\\n| `Open_files`                 | The number of open files.                                    | int  | count   |\\n| `Open_tables`                | The number of of tables that are open.                       | int  | count   |\\n| `Opened_tables`              | The number of tables that have been opened. If Opened_tables is big, your table_open_cache value is probably too small. | int  | count   |\\n| `Qcache_free_blocks`         | The number of free memory blocks in the query cache.         | int  | B       |\\n| `Qcache_free_memory`         | The amount of free memory for the query cache.               | int  | B       |\\n| `Qcache_hits`                | The rate of query cache hits.                                | int  | count   |\\n| `Qcache_inserts`             | The number of queries added to the query cache.              | int  | count   |\\n| `Qcache_lowmem_prunes`       | The number of queries that were deleted from the query cache because of low memory. | int  | count   |\\n| `Qcache_not_cached`          | The number of noncached queries (not cacheable, or not cached due to the query_cache_type setting). | int  | count   |\\n| `Qcache_queries_in_cache`    | The number of queries registered in the query cache.         | int  | count   |\\n| `Qcache_total_blocks`        | The total number of blocks in the query cache.               | int  | count   |\\n| `Queries`                    | The rate of queries.                                         | int  | count   |\\n| `Questions`                  | The rate of statements executed by the server.               | int  | count   |\\n| `Select_full_join`           | The number of joins that perform table scans because they do not use indexes. If this value is not 0, you should carefully check the indexes of your tables. | int  | count   |\\n| `Select_full_range_join`     | The number of joins that used a range search on a reference table. | int  | count   |\\n| `Select_range`               | The number of joins that used ranges on the first table. This is normally not a critical issue even if the value is quite large. | int  | count   |\\n| `Select_range_check`         | The number of joins without keys that check for key usage after each row. If this is not 0, you should carefully check the indexes of your tables. | int  | count   |\\n| `Select_scan`                | The number of joins that did a full scan of the first table. | int  | count   |\\n| `Slow_queries`               | The rate of slow queries.                                    | int  | count   |\\n| `Sort_merge_passes`          | The number of merge passes that the sort algorithm has had to do. If this value is large, you should consider increasing the value of the sort_buffer_size system variable. | int  | count   |\\n| `Sort_range`                 | The number of sorts that were done using ranges.             | int  | count   |\\n| `Sort_rows`                  | The number of sorted rows.                                   | int  | count   |\\n| `Sort_scan`                  | The number of sorts that were done by scanning the table.    | int  | count   |\\n| `Table_locks_immediate`      | The number of times that a request for a table lock could be granted immediately. | int  | count   |\\n| `Table_locks_waited`         | The total number of times that a request for a table lock could not be granted immediately and a wait was needed. | int  | count   |\\n| `Threads_cached`             | The number of threads in the thread cache.                   | int  | count   |\\n| `Threads_connected`          | The number of currently open connections.                    | int  | count   |\\n| `Threads_created`            | The number of threads created to handle connections. If Threads_created is big, you may want to increase the thread_cache_size value. | int  | count   |\\n| `Threads_running`            | The number of threads that are not sleeping.                 | int  | count   |\\n| `max_connections`            | The maximum number of connections that have been in use simultaneously since the server started. | int  | count   |\\n| `query_cache_size`           | The amount of memory allocated for caching query results.    | int  | B       |\\n| `table_open_cache`           | The number of open tables for all threads. Increasing this value increases the number of file descriptors that mysqld requires. | int  | count   |\\n| `thread_cache_size`          | How many threads the server should cache for reuse. When a client disconnects, the client\'s threads are put in the cache if there are fewer than thread_cache_size threads there. | int  | B       |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `mysql_schema`\\n\\n具体字段，以实际采集上来的数据为准，部分字段，会因 MySQL 配置、已有数据等原因，采集不到\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag           | Description             |\\n| :------------ | :---------------------- |\\n| `host`        | The server host address |\\n| `schema_name` | Schema name             |\\n| `server`      | Server addr             |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 字段列表\\n\\n| Metric               | Description                         | Type  | Unit |\\n| :------------------- | :---------------------------------- | ----- | ---- |\\n| `query_run_time_avg` | Avg query response time per schema. | float | ns   |\\n| `schema_size`        | Size of schemas(MiB)                | float | MB   |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `mysql_innodb`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag      | Description             |\\n| :------- | :---------------------- |\\n| `host`   | The server host address |\\n| `server` | Server addr             |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 字段列表\\n\\n| Metric                            | Description                                                  | Type | Unit  |\\n| :-------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ---- | ----- |\\n| `active_transactions`             | The number of active transactions on InnoDB tables           | int  | count |\\n| `adaptive_hash_searches`          | Number of successful searches using Adaptive Hash Index      | int  | count |\\n| `adaptive_hash_searches_btree`    | Number of searches using B-tree on an index search           | int  | count |\\n| `buffer_data_reads`               | Amount of data read in bytes (innodb_data_reads)             | int  | count |\\n| `buffer_data_written`             | Amount of data written in bytes (innodb_data_written)        | int  | count |\\n| `buffer_pages_created`            | Number of pages created (innodb_pages_created)               | int  | count |\\n| `buffer_pages_read`               | Number of pages read (innodb_pages_read)                     | int  | count |\\n| `buffer_pages_written`            | Number of pages written (innodb_pages_written)               | int  | count |\\n| `buffer_pool_bytes_data`          | The total number of bytes in the InnoDB buffer pool containing data. The number includes both dirty and clean pages. | int  | B     |\\n| `buffer_pool_bytes_dirty`         | Buffer bytes containing data (innodb_buffer_pool_bytes_data) | int  | count |\\n| `buffer_pool_pages_data`          | Buffer pages containing data (innodb_buffer_pool_pages_data) | int  | count |\\n| `buffer_pool_pages_dirty`         | Buffer pages currently dirty (innodb_buffer_pool_pages_dirty) | int  | count |\\n| `buffer_pool_pages_flushed`       | The number of requests to flush pages from the InnoDB buffer pool | int  | count |\\n| `buffer_pool_pages_free`          | Buffer pages currently free (innodb_buffer_pool_pages_free)  | int  | count |\\n| `buffer_pool_pages_misc`          | Buffer pages for misc use such as row locks or the adaptive hash index (innodb_buffer_pool_pages_misc) | int  | count |\\n| `buffer_pool_pages_total`         | Total buffer pool size in pages (innodb_buffer_pool_pages_total) | int  | count |\\n| `buffer_pool_read_ahead`          | Number of pages read as read ahead (innodb_buffer_pool_read_ahead) | int  | count |\\n| `buffer_pool_read_ahead_evicted`  | Read-ahead pages evicted without being accessed (innodb_buffer_pool_read_ahead_evicted) | int  | count |\\n| `buffer_pool_read_ahead_rnd`      | The number of random `read-aheads` initiated by InnoDB. This happens when a query scans a large portion of a table but in random order. | int  | count |\\n| `buffer_pool_read_requests`       | Number of logical read requests (innodb_buffer_pool_read_requests) | int  | count |\\n| `buffer_pool_reads`               | Number of reads directly from disk (innodb_buffer_pool_reads) | int  | count |\\n| `buffer_pool_size`                | Server buffer pool size (all buffer pools) in bytes          | int  | count |\\n| `buffer_pool_wait_free`           | Number of times waited for free buffer (innodb_buffer_pool_wait_free) | int  | count |\\n| `buffer_pool_write_requests`      | Number of write requests (innodb_buffer_pool_write_requests) | int  | count |\\n| `checkpoint_age`                  | Checkpoint age as shown in the LOG section of the `SHOW ENGINE INNODB STATUS` output | int  | count |\\n| `current_transactions`            | Current `InnoDB` transactions                                | int  | count |\\n| `data_fsyncs`                     | The number of fsync() operations per second.                 | int  | count |\\n| `data_pending_fsyncs`             | The current number of pending fsync()
```
