As shown in the FILE I/O section of the `SHOW ENGINE INNODB STATUS` output. | int  | count |\\n| `pending_aio_sync_ios`            | As shown in the FILE I/O section of the `SHOW ENGINE INNODB STATUS` output. | int  | count |\\n| `pending_buffer_pool_flushes`     | As shown in the FILE I/O section of the `SHOW ENGINE INNODB STATUS` output. | int  | count |\\n| `pending_checkpoint_writes`       | As shown in the FILE I/O section of the `SHOW ENGINE INNODB STATUS` output. | int  | count |\\n| `pending_ibuf_aio_reads`          | As shown in the FILE I/O section of the `SHOW ENGINE INNODB STATUS` output. | int  | count |\\n| `pending_log_flushes`             | As shown in the FILE I/O section of the `SHOW ENGINE INNODB STATUS` output. | int  | count |\\n| `pending_log_writes`              | As shown in the FILE I/O section of the `SHOW ENGINE INNODB STATUS` output. | int  | count |\\n| `pending_normal_aio_reads`        | As shown in the FILE I/O section of the `SHOW ENGINE INNODB STATUS` output. | int  | count |\\n| `pending_normal_aio_writes`       | As shown in the FILE I/O section of the `SHOW ENGINE INNODB STATUS` output. | int  | count |\\n| `queries_inside`                  | As shown in the FILE I/O section of the `SHOW ENGINE INNODB STATUS` output. | int  | count |\\n| `queries_queued`                  | As shown in the FILE I/O section of the `SHOW ENGINE INNODB STATUS` output. | int  | count |\\n| `read_views`                      | As shown in the FILE I/O section of the `SHOW ENGINE INNODB STATUS` output. | int  | count |\\n| `rows_deleted`                    | Number of rows deleted from InnoDB tables.                   | int  | count |\\n| `rows_inserted`                   | Number of rows inserted into InnoDB tables.                  | int  | count |\\n| `rows_read`                       | Number of rows read from InnoDB tables.                      | int  | count |\\n| `rows_updated`                    | Number of rows updated in InnoDB tables.                     | int  | count |\\n| `s_lock_os_waits`                 | As shown in the SEMAPHORES section of the `SHOW ENGINE INNODB STATUS` output | int  | count |\\n| `s_lock_spin_rounds`              | As shown in the SEMAPHORES section of the `SHOW ENGINE INNODB STATUS` output. | int  | count |\\n| `s_lock_spin_waits`               | As shown in the SEMAPHORES section of the `SHOW ENGINE INNODB STATUS` output. | int  | count |\\n| `semaphore_wait_time`             | Semaphore wait time                                          | int  | count |\\n| `semaphore_waits`                 | The number semaphore currently being waited for by operations on InnoDB tables. | int  | count |\\n| `tables_in_use`                   | Tables in use                                                | int  | count |\\n| `trx_rseg_history_len`            | Length of the TRX_RSEG_HISTORY list                          | int  | count |\\n| `x_lock_os_waits`                 | As shown in the SEMAPHORES section of the `SHOW ENGINE INNODB STATUS` output. | int  | count |\\n| `x_lock_spin_rounds`              | As shown in the SEMAPHORES section of the `SHOW ENGINE INNODB STATUS` output. | int  | count |\\n| `x_lock_spin_waits`               | As shown in the SEMAPHORES section of the `SHOW ENGINE INNODB STATUS` output. | int  | count |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `mysql_table_schema`\\n\\nMySQL 表指标\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag            | Description                                                  |\\n| :------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- |\\n| `engine`       | The storage engine for the table. See The InnoDB Storage Engine, and Alternative Storage Engines. |\\n| `host`         | The server host address                                      |\\n| `server`       | Server addr                                                  |\\n| `table_name`   | The name of the table.                                       |\\n| `table_schema` | The name of the schema (database) to which the table belongs. |\\n| `table_type`   | BASE TABLE for a table, VIEW for a view, or SYSTEM VIEW for an INFORMATION_SCHEMA table. |\\n| `version`      | The version number of the table\'s .frm file.                 |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 字段列表\\n\\n| Metric         | Description                                                  | Type | Unit  |\\n| :------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ---- | ----- |\\n| `data_free`    | The number of rows. Some storage engines, such as MyISAM, store the exact count. For other storage engines, such as InnoDB, this value is an approximation, and may vary from the actual value by as much as 40% to 50%. In such cases, use SELECT COUNT(*) to obtain an accurate count. | int  | count |\\n| `data_length`  | For InnoDB, DATA_LENGTH is the approximate amount of space allocated for the clustered index, in bytes. Specifically, it is the clustered index size, in pages, multiplied by the InnoDB page size | int  | count |\\n| `index_length` | For InnoDB, INDEX_LENGTH is the approximate amount of space allocated for non-clustered indexes, in bytes. Specifically, it is the sum of non-clustered index sizes, in pages, multiplied by the InnoDB page size | int  | count |\\n| `table_rows`   | The number of rows. Some storage engines, such as MyISAM, store the exact count. For other storage engines, such as InnoDB, this value is an approximation, and may vary from the actual value by as much as 40% to 50%. In such cases, use SELECT COUNT(*) to obtain an accurate count. | int  | count |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `mysql_user_status`\\n\\nMySQL 用户指标\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag      | Description                                      |\\n| :------- | :----------------------------------------------- |\\n| `host`   | The server host address                          |\\n| `server` | The server address containing both host and port |\\n| `user`   | user                                             |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 字段列表\\n\\n| Metric                          | Description                                                  | Type | Unit  |\\n| :------------------------------ | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ---- | ----- |\\n| `bytes_received`                | The number of bytes received this user                       | int  | count |\\n| `bytes_sent`                    | The number of bytes sent this user                           | int  | count |\\n| `current_connect`               | The number of current connect                                | int  | count |\\n| `max_execution_time_exceeded`   | The number of SELECT statements for which the execution timeout was exceeded. | int  | count |\\n| `max_execution_time_set`        | The number of SELECT statements for which a nonzero execution timeout was set. This includes statements that include a nonzero MAX_EXECUTION_TIME optimizer hint, and statements that include no such hint but execute while the timeout indicated by the max_execution_time system variable is nonzero. | int  | count |\\n| `max_execution_time_set_failed` | The number of SELECT statements for which the attempt to set an execution timeout failed. | int  | count |\\n| `slow_queries`                  | The number of queries that have taken more than long_query_time seconds. This counter increments regardless of whether the slow query log is enabled | int  | count |\\n| `sort_rows`                     | The number of sorted rows.                                   | int  | count |\\n| `sort_scan`                     | The number of sorts that were done by scanning the table.    | int  | count |\\n| `table_open_cache_hits`         | The number of hits for open tables cache lookups.            | int  | count |\\n| `table_open_cache_misses`       | The number of misses for open tables cache lookups.          | int  | count |\\n| `table_open_cache_overflows`    | The number of overflows for the open tables cache. This is the number of times, after a table is opened or closed, a cache instance has an unused entry and the size of the instance is larger than table_open_cache / table_open_cache_instances. | int  | count |\\n| `total_connect`                 | The number of total connect                                  | int  | count |"} ');
INSERT INTO integrate (id, name, `key`, cate, path, display) VALUES (7, 'Oracle', 'oracle', 1, 'db/oracle.conf', '{
    "instruction": "### 前置条件\\n\\n- 创建监控账号或者直接使用`admin`\\n\\n如果是使用单 PDB 或者非 CDB 实例，一个本地用户(local user)就足够了：\\n\\n```\\n-- Create the datasleuth user. Replace the password placeholder with a secure password.\\nCREATE USER datasleuth IDENTIFIED BY <PASSWORD>;\\n\\n-- Grant access to the datasleuth user.\\nGRANT CONNECT, CREATE SESSION TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT_CATALOG_ROLE to datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON DBA_TABLESPACE_USAGE_METRICS TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON DBA_TABLESPACES TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON DBA_USERS TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON SYS.DBA_DATA_FILES TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON V_$ACTIVE_SESSION_HISTORY TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON V_$ARCHIVE_DEST TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON V_$ASM_DISKGROUP TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON V_$DATABASE TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON V_$DATAFILE TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON V_$INSTANCE TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON V_$LOG TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON V_$OSSTAT TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON V_$PGASTAT TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON V_$PROCESS TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON V_$RECOVERY_FILE_DEST TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON V_$RESTORE_POINT TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON V_$SESSION TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON V_$SGASTAT TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON V_$SYSMETRIC TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON V_$SYSTEM_PARAMETER TO datasleuth;\\n```\\n\\n如果想监控来自 CDB 和所有 PDB 中的表空间(Table Spaces)，需要一个有合适权限的公共用户(common user):\\n\\n```\\n-- Create the datasleuth user. Replace the password placeholder with a secure password.\\nCREATE USER datasleuth IDENTIFIED BY <PASSWORD>;\\n\\n-- Grant access to the datasleuth user.\\nALTER USER datasleuth SET CONTAINER_DATA=ALL CONTAINER=CURRENT;\\nGRANT CONNECT, CREATE SESSION TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT_CATALOG_ROLE to datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON v_$instance TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON v_$database TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON v_$sysmetric TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON v_$system_parameter TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON v_$session TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON v_$recovery_file_dest TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON v_$active_session_history TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON v_$osstat TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON v_$restore_point TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON v_$process TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON v_$datafile TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON v_$pgastat TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON v_$sgastat TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON v_$log TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON v_$archive_dest TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON v_$asm_diskgroup TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON sys.dba_data_files TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON DBA_TABLESPACES TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON DBA_TABLESPACE_USAGE_METRICS TO datasleuth;\\nGRANT SELECT ON DBA_USERS TO datasleuth;\\n```\\n\\n> 注意：上述的 SQL 语句由于 Oracle 版本的原因部分可能会出现 \\"表不存在\\" 等错误，忽略即可。\\n\\n- 安装依赖包\\n\\n根据操作系统和 Oracle 版本选择安装对应的安装包，参考[这里](https://oracle.github.io/odpi/doc/installation.html)，如：\\n\\n```\\nwget https://download.oracle.com/otn_software/linux/instantclient/211000/instantclient-basiclite-linux.x64-21.1.0.0.0.zip\\nunzip instantclient-basiclite-linux.x64-21.1.0.0.0.zip\\n```\\n\\n将解压后的目录文件路径添加到以下配置信息中的 `oracle监控依赖包路径`中。\\n\\n另外，可能还需要安装额外的依赖库：\\n\\n```\\napt-get install -y libaio-dev libaio1\\n```\\n\\n",
    "metrics": "### `oracle_process`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag              | Description         |\\n| :--------------- | :------------------ |\\n| `host`           | Host name           |\\n| `oracle_server`  | Server addr         |\\n| `oracle_service` | Server service      |\\n| `pdb_name`       | PDB name            |\\n| `program`        | Program in progress |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric             | Description                                  | Type  | Unit |\\n| :----------------- | :------------------------------------------- | ----- | ---- |\\n| `pga_alloc_mem`    | PGA memory allocated by process              | float | B    |\\n| `pga_freeable_mem` | PGA memory freeable by process               | float | B    |\\n| `pga_max_mem`      | PGA maximum memory ever allocated by process | float | B    |\\n| `pga_used_mem`     | PGA memory used by process                   | float | B    |\\n| `pid`              | Oracle process identifier                    | int   | -    |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `oracle_tablespace`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag               | Description      |\\n| :---------------- | :--------------- |\\n| `host`            | Host name        |\\n| `oracle_server`   | Server addr      |\\n| `oracle_service`  | Server service   |\\n| `pdb_name`        | PDB name         |\\n| `tablespace_name` | Table space name |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric       | Description         | Type  | Unit  |\\n| :----------- | :------------------ | ----- | ----- |\\n| `in_use`     | Table space in-use  | float | count |\\n| `off_use`    | Table space offline | float | count |\\n| `ts_size`    | Table space size    | float | B     |\\n| `used_space` | Used space          | float | count |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `oracle_system`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag              | Description    |\\n| :--------------- | :------------- |\\n| `host`           | Host name      |\\n| `oracle_server`  | Server addr    |\\n| `oracle_service` | Server service |\\n| `pdb_name`       | PDB name       |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric                     | Description                        | Type  | Unit    |\\n| :------------------------- | :--------------------------------- | ----- | ------- |\\n| `active_sessions`          | Number of active sessions          | float | count   |\\n| `buffer_cachehit_ratio`    | Ratio of buffer cache hits         | float | percent |\\n| `cache_blocks_corrupt`     | Corrupt cache blocks               | float | count   |\\n| `cache_blocks_lost`        | Lost cache blocks                  | float | count   |\\n| `consistent_read_changes`  | Consistent read changes per second | float | count   |\\n| `consistent_read_gets`     | Consistent read gets per second    | float | count   |\\n| `cursor_cachehit_ratio`    | Ratio of cursor cache hits         | float | percent |\\n| `database_cpu_time_ratio`  | Database CPU time ratio            | float | percent |\\n| `database_wait_time_ratio` | Memory sorts per second            | float | percent |\\n| `db_block_changes`         | DB block changes per second        | float | count   |\\n| `db_block_gets`            | DB block gets per second           | float | count   |\\n| `disk_sorts`               | Disk sorts per second              | float | count   |\\n| `enqueue_timeouts`         | Enqueue timeouts per second        | float | count   |\\n| `execute_without_parse`    | Execute without parse ratio        | float | count   |\\n| `gc_cr_block_received`     | GC CR block received               | float | count   |\\n| `host_cpu_utilization`     | Host CPU utilization (%)           | float | percent |\\n| `library_cachehit_ratio`   | Ratio of library cache hits        | float | percent |\\n| `logical_reads`            | Logical reads per second           | float | count   |\\n| `logons`                   | Number of logon attempts           | float | count   |\\n| `memory_sorts_ratio`       | Memory sorts ratio                 | float | percent |\\n| `physical_reads`           | Physical reads per second          | float | count   |\\n| `physical_reads_direct`    | Physical reads direct per second   | float | count   |\\n| `physical_writes`          | Physical writes per second         | float | count   |\\n| `redo_generated`           | Redo generated per second          | float | count   |\\n| `redo_writes`              | Redo writes per second             | float | count   |\\n| `rows_per_sort`            | Rows per sort                      | float | count   |\\n| `service_response_time`    | Service response time              | float | sec     |\\n| `session_count`            | Session count                      | float | count   |\\n| `session_limit_usage`      | Session limit usage                | float | percent |\\n| `shared_pool_free`         | Shared pool free memory %          | float | percent |\\n| `soft_parse_ratio`         | Soft parse ratio                   | float | percent |\\n| `sorts_per_user_call`      | Sorts per user call                | float | count   |\\n| `temp_space_used`          | Temp space used                    | float | B       |\\n| `user_rollbacks`           | Number of user rollbacks           | float | count   |"}');
INSERT INTO integrate (id, name, `key`, cate, path, display) VALUES (8, 'Rabbitmq', 'rabbitmq', 2, 'rabbitmq/rabbitmq.conf', '{
    "instruction": "### 前置条件\\n\\n- RabbitMQ 版本 >= `3.8.14`; 已测试的版本：\\n\\n  - 3.11.x\\n  - 3.10.x\\n  - 3.9.x\\n  - 3.8.x\\n\\n- 安装 `rabbitmq` 以 `Ubuntu` 为例\\n\\n  ```\\n  sudo apt-get update\\n  sudo apt-get install rabbitmq-server\\n  sudo service rabbitmq-server start\\n  ```\\n\\n- 开启 `REST API plug-ins`\\n\\n  ```\\n  sudo rabbitmq-plugins enable rabbitmq-management\\n  ```\\n\\n- 创建 user，比如：\\n\\n  ```\\n  sudo rabbitmqctl add_user guance <SECRET>\\n  sudo rabbitmqctl set_permissions  -p / guance \\"^aliveness-test$\\" \\"^amq\\\\.default$\\" \\".*\\"\\n  sudo rabbitmqctl set_user_tags guance monitoring\\n  ```\\n\\n",
    "metrics": "### `rabbitmq_overview`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag                | Description                      |\\n| :----------------- | :------------------------------- |\\n| `cluster_name`     | RabbitMQ cluster name            |\\n| `host`             | Hostname of RabbitMQ running on. |\\n| `rabbitmq_version` | RabbitMQ version                 |\\n| `url`              | RabbitMQ url                     |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric                                       | Description                                                  | Type  | Unit    |\\n| :------------------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ------- |\\n| `message_ack_count`                          | Number of messages delivered to clients and acknowledged     | int   | count   |\\n| `message_ack_rate`                           | Rate of messages delivered to clients and acknowledged per second | float | percent |\\n| `message_confirm_count`                      | Count of messages confirmed                                  | int   | count   |\\n| `message_confirm_rate`                       | Rate of messages confirmed per second                        | float | percent |\\n| `message_deliver_get_count`                  | Sum of messages delivered in acknowledgement mode to consumers, in no-acknowledgement mode to consumers, in acknowledgement mode in response to basic.get, and in no-acknowledgement mode in response to basic.get | int   | count   |\\n| `message_deliver_get_rate`                   | Rate per second of the sum of messages delivered in acknowledgement mode to consumers, in no-acknowledgement mode to consumers, in acknowledgement mode in response to basic.get, and in no-acknowledgement mode in response to basic.get | float | percent |\\n| `message_publish_count`                      | Count of messages published                                  | int   | count   |\\n| `message_publish_in_count`                   | Count of messages published from channels into this overview | int   | count   |\\n| `message_publish_in_rate`                    | Rate of messages published from channels into this overview per sec | float | percent |\\n| `message_publish_out_count`                  | Count of messages published from this overview into queues   | int   | count   |\\n| `message_publish_out_rate`                   | Rate of messages published from this overview into queues per second | float | percent |\\n| `message_publish_rate`                       | Rate of messages published per second                        | float | percent |\\n| `message_redeliver_count`                    | Count of subset of messages in deliver_get which had the redelivered flag set | int   | count   |\\n| `message_redeliver_rate`                     | Rate of subset of messages in deliver_get which had the redelivered flag set per second | float | percent |\\n| `message_return_unroutable_count`            | Count of messages returned to publisher as unroutable        | int   | count   |\\n| `message_return_unroutable_count_rate`       | Rate of messages returned to publisher as unroutable per second | float | percent |\\n| `object_totals_channels`                     | Total number of channels                                     | int   | count   |\\n| `object_totals_connections`                  | Total number of connections                                  | int   | count   |\\n| `object_totals_consumers`                    | Total number of consumers                                    | int   | count   |\\n| `object_totals_queues`                       | Total number of queues                                       | int   | count   |\\n| `queue_totals_messages_count`                | Total number of messages (ready plus unacknowledged)         | int   | count   |\\n| `queue_totals_messages_rate`                 | Total rate of messages (ready plus unacknowledged)           | float | percent |\\n| `queue_totals_messages_ready_count`          | Number of messages ready for delivery                        | int   | count   |\\n| `queue_totals_messages_ready_rate`           | Rate of number of messages ready for delivery                | float | percent |\\n| `queue_totals_messages_unacknowledged_count` | Number of unacknowledged messages                            | int   | count   |\\n| `queue_totals_messages_unacknowledged_rate`  | Rate of number of unacknowledged messages                    | float | percent |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `rabbitmq_queue`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag          | Description                      |\\n| :----------- | :------------------------------- |\\n| `host`       | Hostname of RabbitMQ running on. |\\n| `node_name`  | RabbitMQ node name               |\\n| `queue_name` | RabbitMQ queue name              |\\n| `url`        | RabbitMQ host URL                |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric                         | Description                                                  | Type  | Unit    |\\n| :----------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ------- |\\n| `bindings_count`               | Number of bindings for a specific queue                      | int   | count   |\\n| `consumer_utilization`         | The ratio of time that a queue\'s consumers can take new messages | float | percent |\\n| `consumers`                    | Number of consumers                                          | int   | count   |\\n| `head_message_timestamp`       | Timestamp of the head message of the queue. Shown as millisecond | int   | msec    |\\n| `memory`                       | Bytes of memory consumed by the Erlang process associated with the queue, including stack, heap and internal structures | int   | B       |\\n| `message_ack_count`            | Number of messages in queues delivered to clients and acknowledged | int   | count   |\\n| `message_ack_rate`             | Number per second of messages delivered to clients and acknowledged | float | percent |\\n| `message_deliver_count`        | Count of messages delivered in acknowledgement mode to consumers | int   | count   |\\n| `message_deliver_get_count`    | Sum of messages in queues delivered in acknowledgement mode to consumers, in no-acknowledgement mode to consumers, in acknowledgement mode in response to basic.get, and in no-acknowledgement mode in response to basic.get. | int   | count   |\\n| `message_deliver_get_rate`     | Rate per second of the sum of messages in queues delivered in acknowledgement mode to consumers, in no-acknowledgement mode to consumers, in acknowledgement mode in response to basic.get, and in no-acknowledgement mode in response to basic.get. | float | percent |\\n| `message_deliver_rate`         | Rate of messages delivered in acknowledgement mode to consumers | float | percent |\\n| `message_publish_count`        | Count of messages in queues published                        | int   | count   |\\n| `message_publish_rate`         | Rate per second of messages published                        | float | percent |\\n| `message_redeliver_count`      | Count of subset of messages in queues in deliver_get which had the redelivered flag set | int   | count   |\\n| `message_redeliver_rate`       | Rate per second of subset of messages in deliver_get which had the redelivered flag set | float | percent |\\n| `messages`                     | Count of the total messages in the queue                     | int   | count   |\\n| `messages_rate`                | Count per second of the total messages in the queue          | float | percent |\\n| `messages_ready`               | Number of messages ready to be delivered to clients          | int   | count   |\\n| `messages_ready_rate`          | Number per second of messages ready to be delivered to clients | float | percent |\\n| `messages_unacknowledged`      | Number of messages delivered to clients but not yet acknowledged | int   | count   |\\n| `messages_unacknowledged_rate` | Number per second of messages delivered to clients but not yet acknowledged | float | percent |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `rabbitmq_exchange`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag             | Description                                                  |\\n| :-------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- |\\n| `auto_delete`   | If set, the exchange is deleted when all queues have finished using it |\\n| `durable`       | If set when creating a new exchange, the exchange will be marked as durable. Durable exchanges remain active when a server restarts. Non-durable exchanges (transient exchanges) are purged if/when a server restarts. |\\n| `exchange_name` | RabbitMQ exchange name                                       |\\n| `host`          | Hostname of RabbitMQ running on.                             |\\n| `internal`      | If set, the exchange may not be used directly by publishers, but only when bound to other exchanges. Internal exchanges are used to construct wiring that is not visible to applications |\\n| `type`          | RabbitMQ exchange type                                       |\\n| `url`           | RabbitMQ host URL                                            |\\n| `vhost`         | RabbitMQ exchange virtual hosts                              |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric                                 | Description                                                  | Type  | Unit    |\\n| :------------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ------- |\\n| `message_ack_count`                    | Number of messages in exchanges delivered to clients and acknowledged | int   | count   |\\n| `message_ack_rate`                     | Rate of messages in exchanges delivered to clients and acknowledged per second | float | percent |\\n| `message_confirm_count`                | Count of messages in exchanges confirmed                     | int   | count   |\\n| `message_confirm_rate`                 | Rate of messages in exchanges confirmed per second           | float | percent |\\n| `message_deliver_get_count`            | Sum of messages in exchanges delivered in acknowledgement mode to consumers, in no-acknowledgement mode to consumers, in acknowledgement mode in response to basic.get, and in no-acknowledgement mode in response to basic.get | int   | count   |\\n| `message_deliver_get_rate`             | Rate per second of the sum of exchange messages delivered in acknowledgement mode to consumers, in no-acknowledgement mode to consumers, in acknowledgement mode in response to basic.get, and in no-acknowledgement mode in response to basic.get | float | percent |\\n| `message_publish_count`                | Count of messages in exchanges published                     | int   | count   |\\n| `message_publish_in_count`             | Count of messages published from channels into this exchange | int   | count   |\\n| `message_publish_in_rate`              | Rate of messages published from channels into this exchange per sec | float | percent |\\n| `message_publish_out_count`            | Count of messages published from this exchange into queues   | int   | count   |\\n| `message_publish_out_rate`             | Rate of messages published from this exchange into queues per second | float | percent |\\n| `message_publish_rate`                 | Rate of messages in exchanges published per second           | float | percent |\\n| `message_redeliver_count`              | Count of subset of messages in exchanges in deliver_get which had the redelivered flag set | int   | count   |\\n| `message_redeliver_rate`               | Rate of subset of messages in exchanges in deliver_get which had the redelivered flag set per second | float | percent |\\n| `message_return_unroutable_count`      | Count of messages in exchanges returned to publisher as un-routable | int   | count   |\\n| `message_return_unroutable_count_rate` | Rate of messages in exchanges returned to publisher as un-routable per second | float | percent |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `rabbitmq_node`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag         | Description                      |\\n| :---------- | :------------------------------- |\\n| `host`      | Hostname of RabbitMQ running on. |\\n| `node_name` | RabbitMQ node name               |\\n| `url`       | RabbitMQ url                     |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric              | Description                                                  | Type  | Unit  |\\n| :------------------ | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ----- |\\n| `disk_free`         | Current free disk space                                      | int   | B     |\\n| `disk_free_alarm`   | Does the node have disk alarm                                | bool  | -     |\\n| `fd_used`           | Used file descriptors                                        | int   | -     |\\n| `io_read_avg_time`  | avg wall time (milliseconds) for each disk read operation in the last statistics interval | float | ms    |\\n| `io_seek_avg_time`  | average wall time (milliseconds) for each seek operation in the last statistics interval | float | ms    |\\n| `io_sync_avg_time`  | average wall time (milliseconds) for each fsync() operation in the last statistics interval | float | ms    |\\n| `io_write_avg_time` | avg wall time (milliseconds) for each disk write operation in the last statistics interval | float | ms    |\\n| `mem_alarm`         | Does the node have mem alarm                                 | bool  | -     |\\n| `mem_limit`         | Memory usage high watermark in bytes                         | int   | B     |\\n| `mem_used`          | Memory used in bytes                                         | int   | B     |\\n| `run_queue`         | Average number of Erlang processes waiting to run            | int   | count |\\n| `running`           | Is the node running or not                                   | bool  | -     |\\n| `sockets_used`      | Number of file descriptors used as sockets                   | int   | count |"}');
INSERT INTO integrate (id, name, `key`, cate, path, display) VALUES (9, 'Redis', 'redis', 1, 'db/redis.conf', '{
    "instruction": "已测试的版本：\\n\\n- 7.0.11\\n- 6.2.12\\n- 5.0.14\\n- 4.0.14\\n\\n### 前置条件\\n\\n- 在采集主从架构下数据时，请配置从节点的主机信息进行数据采集，可以得到主从相关的指标信息。\\n- 创建监控用户：redis 6.0+ 进入 `redis-cli` 命令行，创建用户并且授权：\\n\\n```\\nACL SETUSER username >password\\nACL SETUSER username on +@dangerous\\nACL SETUSER username on +ping\\n```\\n",
    "metrics": "### `redis_bigkey`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag       | Description |\\n| :-------- | :---------- |\\n| `db_name` | db          |\\n| `key`     | monitor key |\\n| `server`  | Server addr |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 字段列表\\n\\n| Metric         | Description | Type | Unit |\\n| :------------- | :---------- | ---- | ---- |\\n| `value_length` | Key length  | int  | -    |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `redis_client`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag      | Description                                                  |\\n| :------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- |\\n| `addr`   | Address without port of the client                           |\\n| `host`   | Hostname                                                     |\\n| `name`   | The name set by the client with `CLIENT SETNAME`, default unknown |\\n| `server` | Server addr                                                  |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 字段列表\\n\\n| Metric | Description                                 | Type | Unit  |\\n| :----- | :------------------------------------------ | ---- | ----- |\\n| `age`  | Total duration of the connection in seconds | int  | count |\\n| `fd`   | File descriptor corresponding to the socket | int  | count |\\n| `idle` | Idle time of the connection in seconds      | int  | count |\\n| `psub` | Number of pattern matching subscriptions    | int  | count |\\n| `sub`  | Number of channel subscriptions             | int  | count |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `redis_cluster`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag      | Description |\\n| :------- | :---------- |\\n| `server` | Server addr |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 字段列表\\n\\n| Metric                            | Description                                                  | Type | Unit  |\\n| :-------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ---- | ----- |\\n| `cluster_current_epoch`           | The local Current Epoch variable. This is used in order to create unique increasing version numbers during fail overs. | int  | -     |\\n| `cluster_known_nodes`             | The total number of known nodes in the cluster, including nodes in HANDSHAKE state that may not currently be proper members of the cluster. | int  | count |\\n| `cluster_my_epoch`                | The Config Epoch of the node we are talking with. This is the current configuration version assigned to this node. | int  | -     |\\n| `cluster_size`                    | The number of master nodes serving at least one hash slot in the cluster. | int  | count |\\n| `cluster_slots_assigned`          | Number of slots which are associated to some node (not unbound). This number should be 16384 for the node to work properly, which means that each hash slot should be mapped to a node. | int  | count |\\n| `cluster_slots_fail`              | Number of hash slots mapping to a node in FAIL state. If this number is not zero the node is not able to serve queries unless cluster-require-full-coverage is set to no in the configuration. | int  | count |\\n| `cluster_slots_ok`                | Number of hash slots mapping to a node not in `FAIL` or `PFAIL` state. | int  | count |\\n| `cluster_slots_pfail`             | Number of hash slots mapping to a node in `PFAIL` state. Note that those hash slots still work correctly, as long as the `PFAIL` state is not promoted to FAIL by the failure detection algorithm. `PFAIL` only means that we are currently not able to talk with the node, but may be just a transient error. | int  | count |\\n| `cluster_state`                   | State is ok if the node is able to receive queries. fail if there is at least one hash slot which is unbound (no node associated), in error state (node serving it is flagged with FAIL flag), or if the majority of masters can\'t be reached by this node. | int  | -     |\\n| `cluster_stats_messages_received` | Number of messages received via the cluster node-to-node binary bus. | int  | count |\\n| `cluster_stats_messages_sent`     | Number of messages sent via the cluster node-to-node binary bus. | int  | count |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `redis_command_stat`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag      | Description  |\\n| :------- | :----------- |\\n| `host`   | Hostname     |\\n| `method` | Command type |\\n| `server` | Server addr  |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 字段列表\\n\\n| Metric          | Description                                        | Type  | Unit  |\\n| :-------------- | :------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ----- |\\n| `calls`         | The number of calls that reached command execution | int   | count |\\n| `usec`          | The total CPU time consumed by these commands      | int   | μs    |\\n| `usec_per_call` | The average CPU consumed per command execution     | float | μs    |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `redis_db`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag  | Description |\\n| :--- | :---------- |\\n| `db` | db name     |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 字段列表\\n\\n| Metric    | Description | Type | Unit |\\n| :-------- | :---------- | ---- | ---- |\\n| `avg_ttl` | avg ttl     | int  | -    |\\n| `expires` | 过期时间    | int  | -    |\\n| `keys`    | key         | int  | -    |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `redis_info`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag             | Description                 |\\n| :-------------- | :-------------------------- |\\n| `host`          | Hostname                    |\\n| `redis_version` | Version of the Redis server |\\n| `server`        | Server addr                 |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 字段列表\\n\\n| Metric                        | Description                                                  | Type  | Unit    |\\n| :---------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ------- |\\n| `active_defrag_hits`          | Number of value reallocations performed by active the defragmentation process | int   | count   |\\n| `active_defrag_key_hits`      | Number of keys that were actively defragmented               | int   | count   |\\n| `active_defrag_key_misses`    | Number of keys that were skipped by the active defragmentation process | int   | count   |\\n| `active_defrag_misses`        | Number of aborted value reallocations started by the active defragmentation process | int   | count   |\\n| `active_defrag_running`       | Flag indicating if active defragmentation is active          | bool  | count   |\\n| `aof_buffer_length`           | Size of the AOF buffer                                       | float | B       |\\n| `aof_current_size`            | AOF current file size                                        | float | B       |\\n| `aof_last_rewrite_time_sec`   | Duration of the last AOF rewrite operation in seconds        | int   | count   |\\n| `aof_rewrite_in_progress`     | Flag indicating a AOF rewrite operation is on-going          | bool  | count   |\\n| `blocked_clients`             | Number of clients pending on a blocking call (`BLPOP/BRPOP/BRPOPLPUSH/BLMOVE/BZPOPMIN/BZPOPMAX`) | int   | count   |\\n| `client_biggest_input_buf`    | Biggest input buffer among current client connections        | int   | B       |\\n| `client_longest_output_list`  | Longest output list among current client connections         | int   | count   |\\n| `connected_clients`           | Number of client connections (excluding connections from replicas) | int   | count   |\\n| `connected_slaves`            | Number of connected replicas                                 | int   | count   |\\n| `evicted_keys`                | Number of evicted keys due to Max-Memory limit               | int   | count   |\\n| `expired_keys`                | Total number of key expiration events                        | int   | count   |\\n| `info_latency_ms`             | The latency of the redis INFO command.                       | float | ms      |\\n| `keyspace_hits`               | Number of successful lookup of keys in the main dictionary   | int   | count   |\\n| `keyspace_misses`             | Number of failed lookup of keys in the main dictionary       | int   | count   |\\n| `latest_fork_usec`            | Duration of the latest fork operation in microseconds        | int   | ms      |\\n| `loading_eta_seconds`         | ETA in seconds for the load to be complete                   | int   | s       |\\n| `loading_loaded_bytes`        | Number of bytes already loaded                               | float | B       |\\n| `loading_loaded_perc`         | Same value expressed as a percentage                         | float | percent |\\n| `loading_total_bytes`         | Total file size                                              | float | B       |\\n| `master_last_io_seconds_ago`  | Number of seconds since the last interaction with master     | int   | s       |\\n| `master_repl_offset`          | The server\'s current replication offset                      | int   | count   |\\n| `master_sync_in_progress`     | Indicate the master is syncing to the replica                | bool  | -       |\\n| `master_sync_left_bytes`      | Number of bytes left before syncing is complete (may be negative when master_sync_total_bytes is 0) | float | B       |\\n| `maxmemory`                   | The value of the Max Memory configuration directive          | float | B       |\\n| `mem_fragmentation_ratio`     | Ratio between used_memory_rss and used_memory                | float | percent |\\n| `pubsub_channels`             | Global number of pub/sub channels with client subscriptions  | int   | count   |\\n| `pubsub_patterns`             | Global number of pub/sub pattern with client subscriptions   | int   | count   |\\n| `rdb_bgsave_in_progress`      | Flag indicating a RDB save is on-going                       | bool  | -       |\\n| `rdb_changes_since_last_save` | Refers to the number of operations that produced some kind of changes in the dataset since the last time either `SAVE` or `BGSAVE` was called. | int   | count   |\\n| `rdb_last_bgsave_time_sec`    | Duration of the last RDB save operation in seconds           | int   | s       |\\n| `rejected_connections`        | Number of connections rejected because of Max-Clients limit  | int   | count   |\\n| `repl_backlog_histlen`        | Size in bytes of the data in the replication backlog buffer  | float | B       |\\n| `slave_repl_offset`           | The replication offset of the replica instance               | int   | count   |\\n| `total_net_input_bytes`       | The total number of bytes read from the network              | int   | count   |\\n| `total_net_output_bytes`      | The total number of bytes written to the network             | int   | count   |\\n| `used_cpu_sys`                | System CPU consumed by the Redis server, which is the sum of system CPU consumed by all threads of the server process (main thread and background threads) | float | s       |\\n| `used_cpu_sys_children`       | System CPU consumed by the background processes              | float | s       |\\n| `used_cpu_sys_percent`        | System CPU percentage consumed by the Redis server, which is the sum of system CPU consumed by all threads of the server process (main thread and background threads) | float | percent |\\n| `used_cpu_user`               | User CPU consumed by the Redis server, which is the sum of user CPU consumed by all threads of the server process (main thread and background threads) | float | s       |\\n| `used_cpu_user_children`      | User CPU consumed by the background processes                | float | s       |\\n| `used_cpu_user_percent`       | User CPU percentage consumed by the Redis server, which is the sum of user CPU consumed by all threads of the server process (main thread and background threads) | float | percent |\\n| `used_memory`                 | Total number of bytes allocated by Redis using its allocator (either standard libc, jemalloc, or an alternative allocator such as tcmalloc) | float | B       |\\n| `used_memory_lua`             | Number of bytes used by the Lua engine                       | float | B       |\\n| `used_memory_overhead`        | The sum in bytes of all overheads that the server allocated for managing its internal data structures | float | B       |\\n| `used_memory_peak`            | Peak memory consumed by Redis (in bytes)                     | float | B       |\\n| `used_memory_rss`             | Number of bytes that Redis allocated as seen by the operating system (a.k.a resident set size) | float | B       |\\n| `used_memory_startup`         | Initial amount of memory consumed by Redis at startup in bytes | float | B       |"}');
INSERT INTO integrate (id, name, `key`, cate, path, display) VALUES (10, 'Container', 'container', 2, 'container/container.conf', '{
    "instruction": "### 前置条件\\n\\n- 目前 container 支持 Docker、Containerd、CRI-O 容器运行时\\n  - 版本要求：Docker v17.04 及以上版本，Containerd v1.5.1 及以上，CRI-O 1.20.1 及以上\\n\\n\\n### Docker 和 Containerd sock 文件配置\\n\\n如果采集 Docker 数据，则需要填写 Dockerd 地址，默认地址为`unix:///var/run/docker.sock`。\\n\\n如果采集 Containerd 数据，则需要添加 containerd 地址，默认地址为`/var/run/containerd/containerd.sock`。\\n\\n请根据需求选择填写其中某一项或多项。",
    "metrics": "### `docker_containers`\\n\\nThe metric of containers, only supported Running status.\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag                      | Description                                                  |\\n| :----------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- |\\n| `container_id`           | Container ID                                                 |\\n| `container_name`         | Container name from k8s (label `io.kubernetes.container.name`). If empty then use $container_runtime_name. |\\n| `container_runtime`      | Container runtime (this container from Docker/Containerd/cri-o). |\\n| `container_runtime_name` | Container name from runtime (like \'docker ps\'). If empty then use \'unknown\' ([ Version-1.4.6](https://docs.guance.com/datasleuth/changelog/#cl-1.4.6)). |\\n| `container_type`         | The type of the container (this container is created by Kubernetes/Docker/Containerd/cri-o). |\\n| `daemonset`              | The name of the DaemonSet which the object belongs to.       |\\n| `deployment`             | The name of the Deployment which the object belongs to.      |\\n| `image`                  | The full name of the container image, example `nginx.org/nginx:1.21.0`. |\\n| `image_name`             | The name of the container image, example `nginx.org/nginx`.  |\\n| `image_short_name`       | The short name of the container image, example `nginx`.      |\\n| `image_tag`              | The tag of the container image, example `1.21.0`.            |\\n| `namespace`              | The namespace of the container (label `io.kubernetes.pod.namespace`). |\\n| `pod_name`               | The pod name of the container (label `io.kubernetes.pod.name`). |\\n| `pod_uid`                | The pod uid of the container (label `io.kubernetes.pod.uid`). |\\n| `state`                  | Container status (only Running).                             |\\n| `statefulset`            | The name of the StatefulSet which the object belongs to.     |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric                        | Description                                                  | Type  | Unit    |\\n| :---------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ------- |\\n| `block_read_byte`             | Total number of bytes read from the container file system (only supported docker). | int   | B       |\\n| `block_write_byte`            | Total number of bytes wrote to the container file system (only supported docker). | int   | B       |\\n| `cpu_numbers`                 | The number of the CPU core.                                  | int   | count   |\\n| `cpu_usage`                   | The percentage usage of CPU on system host.                  | float | percent |\\n| `cpu_usage_base100`           | The normalized cpu usage, with a maximum of 100%.            | float | percent |\\n| `mem_capacity`                | The total memory in the host machine.                        | int   | B       |\\n| `mem_limit`                   | The limit memory in the container.                           | int   | B       |\\n| `mem_usage`                   | The usage of the memory.                                     | int   | B       |\\n| `mem_used_percent`            | The percentage usage of the memory is calculated based on the capacity of host machine. | float | percent |\\n| `mem_used_percent_base_limit` | The percentage usage of the memory is calculated based on the limit. | float | percent |\\n| `network_bytes_rcvd`          | Total number of bytes received from the network (only count the usage of the main process in the container, excluding loopback). | int   | B       |\\n| `network_bytes_sent`          | Total number of bytes send to the network (only count the usage of the main process in the container, excluding loopback). | int   | B       |"}');
INSERT INTO integrate (id, name, `key`, cate, path, display) VALUES (11, 'Minio', 'minio', 1, 'prom/prom-minio.conf', '{
    "instruction": "### 版本支持\\n\\n- MinIO 版本：ALL\\n\\n说明：示例 MinIO 版本为 RELEASE.2022-06-25T15-50-16Z (commit-id=[bd099f5](https://github.com/facelessuser/pymdown-extensions/commit/bd099f5e71d0ea511846372869bfcb280a5da2f6))\\n\\n### 指标采集\\n\\nMinIO 默认已暴露 [metric](https://docs.min.io/minio/baremetal/monitoring/metrics-alerts/collect-minio-metrics-using-prometheus.html?ref=con#minio-metrics-collect-using-prometheus) ，可以直接采集相关指标。\\n\\n1、 使用`minio-client`（简称`mc`）创建授权信息\\n\\n```\\n$ mc alias set myminio http://172.30.95.233:9000 <user> <password>\\n\\n$ mc admin prometheus generate myminio\\n\\nscrape_configs:\\n- job_name: minio-job\\n  bearer_token: eyJhbGciOiJIUzUxMiIsInR5cCI6IkpXVCJ9.eyJpc3MiOiJwcm9tZXRoZXVzIiwic3ViIjoiVjF4TWMzUkM0U0Nxbm9HdyIsImV4cCI6NDg0NjE5OTk4Nn0.2tuaqgkfzAPnEeoNRHLzO_CsFfhqwRhNCZNPh8ieByMfskT792ouAoebd4SRdTBPyOdIO82xrC09iLcdNpG5OA\\n  metrics_path: /minio/v2/metrics/cluster\\n  scheme: http\\n  static_configs:\\n  - targets: [\'172.30.95.233:9000\']\\n```\\n\\nMinio 提供了通过 `mc` 来生成`token` 信息。输出信息包含了 `bearer_token`、`metrics_path`、`scheme`以及`targets`，通过这些信息可以进行拼装最终的 url 。\\n",
    "metrics": "| 指标                              | 含义                    |\\n| :-------------------------------- | :---------------------- |\\n| node_process_uptime_seconds       | 节点在线时长            |\\n| node_disk_free_bytes              | 节点空间空闲大小        |\\n| node_disk_used_bytes              | 节点空间使用大小        |\\n| node_file_descriptor_open_total   | 节点文件描述打开次数    |\\n| node_go_routine_total             | 节点 go_routine 次数    |\\n| cluster_disk_online_total         | 集群磁盘在线数          |\\n| cluster_disk_offline_total        | 集群磁盘离线数          |\\n| bucket_usage_object_total         | bucket 已用对象数       |\\n| bucket_usage_total_bytes          | bucket 已用字节         |\\n| bucket_objects_size_distribution  | bucket 对象大小区间分布 |\\n| s3_traffic_received_bytes         | s3 接收流量             |\\n| s3_traffic_sent_bytes             | s3 发送流量             |\\n| s3_requests_total                 | s3 请求总数             |\\n| s3_requests_waiting_total         | s3 正在等待请求数       |\\n| s3_requests_errors_total          | s3 异常总数             |\\n| s3_requests_4xx_errors_total      | s3 4xx 异常数           |\\n| s3_time_ttfb_seconds_distribution | s3 TTFB                 |\\n| usage_last_activity_nano_seconds  | 自上使用活动以来的时间  |"}');
INSERT INTO integrate (id, name, `key`, cate, path, display) VALUES (12, 'PostgreSQL', 'postgresql', 1, 'db/postgresql.conf', '{
    "instruction": "### 前置条件\\n\\n- PostgreSQL 版本 >= 9.0\\n- 创建监控帐号或直接使用管理员用户 \\n\\n```\\n-- PostgreSQL >= 10\\ncreate user datasleuth with password \'<PASSWORD>\';\\ngrant pg_monitor to datasleuth;\\ngrant SELECT ON pg_stat_database to datasleuth;\\n\\n-- PostgreSQL < 10\\ncreate user datasleuth with password \'<PASSWORD>\';\\ngrant SELECT ON pg_stat_database to datasleuth;\\n```\\n\\n",
    "metrics": "### `postgresql`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag      | Description        |\\n| :------- | :----------------- |\\n| `db`     | The database name  |\\n| `server` | The server address |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric                     | Description                                                  | Type  | Unit  |\\n| :------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ----- |\\n| `active_time`              | Time spent executing SQL statements in this database, in milliseconds. | float | count |\\n| `blks_hit`                 | The number of times disk blocks were found in the buffer cache, preventing the need to read from the database. | int   | count |\\n| `blks_read`                | The number of disk blocks read in this database.             | int   | count |\\n| `database_size`            | The disk space used by this database.                        | int   | count |\\n| `deadlocks`                | The number of deadlocks detected in this database.           | int   | count |\\n| `idle_in_transaction_time` | Time spent idling while in a transaction in this database, in milliseconds. | float | count |\\n| `numbackends`              | The number of active connections to this database.           | int   | count |\\n| `session_time`             | Time spent by database sessions in this database, in milliseconds. | float | count |\\n| `sessions`                 | Total number of sessions established to this database.       | int   | count |\\n| `sessions_abandoned`       | Number of database sessions to this database that were terminated because connection to the client was lost. | int   | count |\\n| `sessions_fatal`           | Number of database sessions to this database that were terminated by fatal errors. | int   | count |\\n| `sessions_killed`          | Number of database sessions to this database that were terminated by operator intervention. | int   | count |\\n| `temp_bytes`               | The amount of data written to temporary files by queries in this database. | int   | count |\\n| `temp_files`               | The number of temporary files created by queries in this database. | int   | count |\\n| `tup_deleted`              | The number of rows deleted by queries in this database.      | int   | count |\\n| `tup_fetched`              | The number of rows fetched by queries in this database.      | int   | count |\\n| `tup_inserted`             | The number of rows inserted by queries in this database.     | int   | count |\\n| `tup_returned`             | The number of rows returned by queries in this database.     | int   | count |\\n| `tup_updated`              | The number of rows updated by queries in this database.      | int   | count |\\n| `wraparound`               | The number of transactions that can occur until a transaction wraparound. | float | count |\\n| `xact_commit`              | The number of transactions that have been committed in this database. | int   | count |\\n| `xact_rollback`            | The number of transactions that have been rolled back in this database. | int   | count |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `postgresql_lock`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag        | Description        |\\n| :--------- | :----------------- |\\n| `db`       | The database name  |\\n| `locktype` | The lock type      |\\n| `mode`     | The lock mode      |\\n| `schema`   | The schema name    |\\n| `server`   | The server address |\\n| `table`    | The table name     |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric       | Description                                   | Type | Unit  |\\n| :----------- | :-------------------------------------------- | ---- | ----- |\\n| `lock_count` | The number of locks active for this database. | int  | count |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `postgresql_index`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag      | Description        |\\n| :------- | :----------------- |\\n| `db`     | The database name  |\\n| `index`  | The index name     |\\n| `schema` | The schema name    |\\n| `server` | The server address |\\n| `table`  | The table name     |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric          | Description                                                  | Type | Unit  |\\n| :-------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ---- | ----- |\\n| `idx_scan`      | The number of index scans initiated on this table, tagged by index. | int  | count |\\n| `idx_tup_fetch` | The number of live rows fetched by index scans.              | int  | count |\\n| `idx_tup_read`  | The number of index entries returned by scans on this index. | int  | count |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `postgresql_replication`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag      | Description        |\\n| :------- | :----------------- |\\n| `db`     | The database name  |\\n| `server` | The server address |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric                    | Description                                                  | Type | Unit |\\n| :------------------------ | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ---- | ---- |\\n| `replication_delay`       | The current replication delay in seconds. Only available with `postgresql` 9.1 and newer. | int  | s    |\\n| `replication_delay_bytes` | The current replication delay in bytes. Only available with `postgresql` 9.2 and newer. | int  | B    |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `postgresql_size`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag      | Description        |\\n| :------- | :----------------- |\\n| `db`     | The database name  |\\n| `schema` | The schema name    |\\n| `server` | The server address |\\n| `table`  | The table name     |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric       | Description                                                  | Type | Unit |\\n| :----------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ---- | ---- |\\n| `index_size` | The total disk space used by indexes attached to the specified table. | int  | B    |\\n| `table_size` | The total disk space used by the specified table. Includes TOAST, free space map, and visibility map. Excludes indexes. | int  | B    |\\n| `total_size` | The total disk space used by the table, including indexes and TOAST data. | int  | B    |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `postgresql_statio`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag      | Description        |\\n| :------- | :----------------- |\\n| `db`     | The database name  |\\n| `schema` | The schema name    |\\n| `server` | The server address |\\n| `table`  | The table name     |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric            | Description                                                  | Type | Unit  |\\n| :---------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ---- | ----- |\\n| `heap_blks_hit`   | The number of buffer hits in this table.                     | int  | count |\\n| `heap_blks_read`  | The number of disk blocks read from this table.              | int  | count |\\n| `idx_blks_hit`    | The number of buffer hits in all indexes on this table.      | int  | count |\\n| `idx_blks_read`   | The number of disk blocks read from all indexes on this table. | int  | count |\\n| `tidx_blks_hit`   | The number of buffer hits in this table\'s TOAST table index. | int  | count |\\n| `tidx_blks_read`  | The number of disk blocks read from this table\'s TOAST table index. | int  | count |\\n| `toast_blks_hit`  | The number of buffer hits in this table\'s TOAST table.       | int  | count |\\n| `toast_blks_read` | The number of disk blocks read from this table\'s TOAST table. | int  | count |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `postgresql_stat`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag      | Description        |\\n| :------- | :----------------- |\\n| `db`     | The database name  |\\n| `schema` | The schema name    |\\n| `server` | The server address |\\n| `table`  | The table name     |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric              | Description                                                  | Type | Unit  |\\n| :------------------ | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ---- | ----- |\\n| `analyze_count`     | The number of times this table has been manually analyzed.   | int  | count |\\n| `autoanalyze_count` | The number of times this table has been analyzed by the `autovacuum` daemon. | int  | count |\\n| `autovacuum_count`  | The number of times this table has been vacuumed by the `autovacuum` daemon. | int  | count |\\n| `idx_scan`          | The number of index scans initiated on this table, tagged by index. | int  | count |\\n| `idx_tup_fetch`     | The number of live rows fetched by index scans.              | int  | count |\\n| `n_dead_tup`        | The estimated number of dead rows.                           | int  | count |\\n| `n_live_tup`        | The estimated number of live rows.                           | int  | count |\\n| `n_tup_del`         | The number of rows deleted by queries in this database.      | int  | count |\\n| `n_tup_hot_upd`     | The number of rows HOT updated, meaning no separate index update was needed. | int  | count |\\n| `n_tup_ins`         | The number of rows inserted by queries in this database.     | int  | count |\\n| `n_tup_upd`         | The number of rows updated by queries in this database.      | int  | count |\\n| `seq_scan`          | The number of sequential scans initiated on this table.      | int  | count |\\n| `seq_tup_read`      | The number of live rows fetched by sequential scans.         | int  | count |\\n| `vacuum_count`      | The number of times this table has been manually vacuumed.   | int  | count |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `postgresql_slru`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag      | Description            |\\n| :------- | :--------------------- |\\n| `db`     | The database name      |\\n| `name`   | The name of the `SLRU` |\\n| `server` | The server address     |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric         | Description                                                  | Type | Unit  |\\n| :------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ---- | ----- |\\n| `blks_exists`  | Number of blocks checked for existence for this `SLRU` (simple least-recently-used) cache. | int  | count |\\n| `blks_hit`     | Number of times disk blocks were found already in the `SLRU` (simple least-recently-used.) | int  | count |\\n| `blks_read`    | Number of disk blocks read for this `SLRU` (simple least-recently-used) cache. `SLRU` caches are created with a fixed number of pages. | int  | count |\\n| `blks_written` | Number of disk blocks written for this `SLRU` (simple least-recently-used) cache. | int  | count |\\n| `blks_zeroed`  | Number of blocks zeroed during initializations of `SLRU` (simple least-recently-used) cache. | int  | count |\\n| `flushes`      | Number of flush of dirty data for this `SLRU` (simple least-recently-used) cache. | int  | count |\\n| `truncates`    | Number of truncates for this `SLRU` (simple least-recently-used) cache. | int  | count |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `postgresql_bgwriter`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag      | Description        |\\n| :------- | :----------------- |\\n| `db`     | The database name  |\\n| `server` | The server address |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric                  | Description                                                  | Type  | Unit  |\\n| :---------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ----- |\\n| `buffers_alloc`         | The number of buffers allocated                              | int   | count |\\n| `buffers_backend`       | The number of buffers written directly by a backend.         | int   | count |\\n| `buffers_backend_fsync` | The of times a backend had to execute its own fsync call instead of the background writer. | int   | count |\\n| `buffers_checkpoint`    | The number of buffers written during checkpoints.            | int   | count |\\n| `buffers_clean`         | The number of buffers written by the background writer.      | int   | count |\\n| `checkpoint_sync_time`  | The total amount of checkpoint processing time spent synchronizing files to disk. | float | ms    |\\n| `checkpoint_write_time` | The total amount of checkpoint processing time spent writing files to disk. | float | ms    |\\n| `checkpoints_req`       | The number of requested checkpoints that were performed.     | int   | count |\\n| `checkpoints_timed`     | The number of scheduled checkpoints that were performed.     | int   | count |\\n| `maxwritten_clean`      | The number of times the background writer stopped a cleaning scan due to writing too many buffers. | int   | count |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `postgresql_connection`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag      | Description        |\\n| :------- | :----------------- |\\n| `db`     | The database name  |\\n| `server` | The server address |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric                      | Description                                                  | Type  | Unit  |\\n| :-------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ----- | ----- |\\n| `max_connections`           | The maximum number of client connections allowed to this database. | float | count |\\n| `percent_usage_connections` | The number of connections to this database as a fraction of the maximum number of allowed connections. | float | count |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `postgresql_conflict`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag      | Description        |\\n| :------- | :----------------- |\\n| `db`     | The database name  |\\n| `server` | The server address |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric             | Description                                                  | Type | Unit  |\\n| :----------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | ---- | ----- |\\n| `confl_bufferpin`  | Number of queries in this database that have been canceled due to pinned buffers. | int  | count |\\n| `confl_deadlock`   | Number of queries in this database that have been canceled due to deadlocks. | int  | count |\\n| `confl_lock`       | Number of queries in this database that have been canceled due to dropped tablespaces. This will occur when a `temp_tablespace` is dropped while being used on a standby. | int  | count |\\n| `confl_snapshot`   | Number of queries in this database that have been canceled due to old snapshots. | int  | count |\\n| `confl_tablespace` | Number of queries in this database that have been canceled due to dropped tablespaces. This will occur when a `temp_tablespace` is dropped while being used on a standby. | int  | count |\\n\\n\\n\\n### `postgresql_archiver`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag      | Description        |\\n| :------- | :----------------- |\\n| `db`     | The database name  |\\n| `server` | The server address |\\n\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric                  | Description                                               | Type | Unit  |\\n| :---------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------------- | ---- | ----- |\\n| `archived_count`        | Number of WAL files that have been successfully archived. | int  | count |\\n| `archived_failed_count` | Number of failed attempts for archiving WAL files.        | int  | count |"}');
INSERT INTO integrate (id, name, `key`, cate, path, display) VALUES (13, 'Statsd', 'statsd', 2, 'statsd/statsd.conf', '{
    "instruction": "# 概述\\r\\nStatsD 数据接入，会自动开启 StatsD 服务端。然后 DDTrace Agent 可以将采集的指标数据推送到此服务端地址。\\r\\n\\r\\n其中包括 JVM 运行时的 CPU 、内存、线程、类加载信息，也包括开启的各种采集上来的 JMX 指标， 如： Kafka、Tomcat、RabbitMQ 等。\\r\\n\\r\\nStatsd 更多信息参考[这里](https://github.com/statsd/statsd)。\\r\\n\\r\\n## DDTrace Agent \\r\\nDD Trace Agent 的通用接入可参考[这里](https://docs.guance.com/integrations/ddtrace-java/)。\\r\\n\\r\\n支持 Java、Golang、Python、C++ 等多种语言。\\r\\n\\r\\n## tomcat\\r\\n- 已测试的版本：\\r\\n    - 11.0.0\\r\\n    - 10.1.10\\r\\n    - 9.0.76\\r\\n    - 8.5.90\\r\\n- 下载 `dd-java-agent.jar` 包，参见 [这里](https://static.guance.com/dd-image/dd-java-agent.jar);\\r\\n- Tomcat 侧：\\r\\n\\r\\n在 */usr/local/tomcat/bin* 下创建文件 *setenv.sh* 并赋予执行权限，再写入以下内容：\\r\\n\\r\\n```\\r\\nexport CATALINA_OPTS=\\"-javaagent:dd-java-agent.jar \\\\\\r\\n                      -Ddd.jmxfetch.enabled=true \\\\\\r\\n                      -Ddd.jmxfetch.statsd.host=${STATSD_HOST} \\\\\\r\\n                      -Ddd.jmxfetch.statsd.port=${STATSD_PORT} \\\\\\r\\n                      -Ddd.jmxfetch.tomcat.enabled=true\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n参数说明如下：\\r\\n\\r\\n- `javaagent`: 这个填写 `dd-java-agent.jar` 的完整路径；\\r\\n- `Ddd.jmxfetch.enabled`: 填 `true`, 表示开启 dd-trace 的采集功能；\\r\\n- `Ddd.jmxfetch.statsd.host`: 填写 datasleuth 监听的网络地址。不含端口号；\\r\\n- `Ddd.jmxfetch.statsd.port`: 填写 datasleuth 监听的端口号。一般为 `8125`，由 datasleuth 侧的配置决定；\\r\\n- `Ddd.jmxfetch.tomcat.enabled`: 填 `true`, 表示开启 dd-trace 的 Tomcat 采集功能。开启后会多出名为 `tomcat` 的指标集；\\r\\n\\r\\n重启 Tomcat 使配置生效。",
    "metrics": ""}');
INSERT INTO integrate (id, name, `key`, cate, path, display) VALUES (14, 'Kafka', 'kafka', 2, 'db/kafka.conf', '{
    "instruction": "### 前置条件\\n\\n- 安装或下载 [Jolokia](https://search.maven.org/remotecontent?filepath=org/jolokia/jolokia-jvm/1.6.2/jolokia-jvm-1.6.2-agent.jar)。\\n\\nJolokia 是作为 Kafka 的 Java agent，基于 HTTP 协议提供了一个使用 JSON 作为数据格式的外部接口，提供给 datasleuth 使用。 Kafka 启动时，先配置 `KAFKA_OPTS` 环境变量：(port 可根据实际情况修改成可用端口）\\n\\n```\\nexport KAFKA_OPTS=\'$KAFKA_OPTS -javaagent:/usr/local/datasleuth/data/jolokia-jvm-agent.jar=host=*,port=8080\'\\n```\\n\\n另外，也可以单独启动 Jolokia，将其指向 Kafka 进程 PID：\\n\\n```\\njava -jar </path/to/jolokia-jvm-agent.jar> --host 127.0.0.1 --port=8080 start <Kafka-PID>\\n```\\n\\n在开启 Kafka 服务后，如需采集 Producer/Consumer/Connector 指标，则需分别为其配置 Jolokia。",
    "metrics": "### `kafka_controller`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag               | Description            |\\n| :---------------- | ---------------------- |\\n| jolokia_agent_url | Jolokia agent url path |\\n\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| Metric | Description | Type | Unit |\\n| :----- | ----------- | ---- | ---- |\\n| ActiveControllerCount.Value |  | int | count |\\n| AutoLeaderBalanceRateAndTimeMs.50thPercentile |  | float | - |\\n| AutoLeaderBalanceRateAndTimeMs.75thPercentile |  | float | - |\\n| AutoLeaderBalanceRateAndTimeMs.95thPercentile |  | float | - |\\n| AutoLeaderBalanceRateAndTimeMs.98thPercentile |  | float | - |\\n| AutoLeaderBalanceRateAndTimeMs.999thPercentile |  | float | - |\\n| AutoLeaderBalanceRateAndTimeMs.99thPercentile |  | float | - |\\n| AutoLeaderBalanceRateAndTimeMs.Count |  | int | count |\\n| AutoLeaderBalanceRateAndTimeMs.EventType |  | string | - |\\n| AutoLeaderBalanceRateAndTimeMs.FifteenMinuteRate |  | float | - |\\n| AutoLeaderBalanceRateAndTimeMs.FiveMinuteRate |  | float | - |\\n| AutoLeaderBalanceRateAndTimeMs.LatencyUnit |  | string | - |\\n| AutoLeaderBalanceRateAndTimeMs.Max |  | float | - |\\n| AutoLeaderBalanceRateAndTimeMs.Mean |  | float | - |\\n| AutoLeaderBalanceRateAndTimeMs.MeanRate |  | float | - |\\n| AutoLeaderBalanceRateAndTimeMs.Min |  | float | - |\\n| AutoLeaderBalanceRateAndTimeMs.OneMinuteRate |  | float | - |\\n| AutoLeaderBalanceRateAndTimeMs.RateUnit |  | string | - |\\n| AutoLeaderBalanceRateAndTimeMs.StdDev |  | float | - |\\n| ControlledShutdownRateAndTimeMs.50thPercentile |  | float | - |\\n| ControlledShutdownRateAndTimeMs.75thPercentile |  | float | - |\\n| ControlledShutdownRateAndTimeMs.95thPercentile |  | float | - |\\n| ControlledShutdownRateAndTimeMs.98thPercentile |  | float | - |\\n| ControlledShutdownRateAndTimeMs.999thPercentile |  | float | - |\\n| ControlledShutdownRateAndTimeMs.99thPercentile |  | float | - |\\n| ControlledShutdownRateAndTimeMs.Count |  | int | count |\\n| ControlledShutdownRateAndTimeMs.EventType |  | string | - |\\n| ControlledShutdownRateAndTimeMs.FifteenMinuteRate |  | float | - |\\n| ControlledShutdownRateAndTimeMs.FiveMinuteRate |  | float | - |\\n| ControlledShutdownRateAndTimeMs.LatencyUnit |  | string | - |\\n| ControlledShutdownRateAndTimeMs.Max |  | float | - |\\n| ControlledShutdownRateAndTimeMs.Mean |  | float | - |\\n| ControlledShutdownRateAndTimeMs.MeanRate |  | float | - |\\n| ControlledShutdownRateAndTimeMs.Min |  | float | - |\\n| ControlledShutdownRateAndTimeMs.OneMinuteRate |  | float | - |\\n| ControlledShutdownRateAndTimeMs.RateUnit |  | string | - |\\n| ControlledShutdownRateAndTimeMs.StdDev |  | float | - |\\n| ControllerChangeRateAndTimeMs.50thPercentile |  | float | - |\\n| ControllerChangeRateAndTimeMs.75thPercentile |  | float | - |\\n| ControllerChangeRateAndTimeMs.95thPercentile |  | float | - |\\n| ControllerChangeRateAndTimeMs.98thPercentile |  | float | - |\\n| ControllerChangeRateAndTimeMs.999thPercentile |  | float | - |\\n| ControllerChangeRateAndTimeMs.99thPercentile |  | float | - |\\n|
