-|\\n|failed_reauthentication_total |  | int | count|\\n|incoming_byte_rate |  | float | -|\\n|incoming_byte_total |  | int | -|\\n|io_ratio |  | float | -|\\n|io_time_ns_avg |  | float | ns|\\n|io_wait_ratio |  | float | -|\\n|io_wait_time_ns_avg |  | int | ns|\\n|io_waittime_total |  | int | ns|\\n|iotime_total |  | int | ns|\\n|metadata_age |  | float | -|\\n|network_io_rate |  | float | -|\\n|network_io_total |  | int | count|\\n|outgoing_byte_rate |  | float | -|\\n|outgoing_byte_total |  | int | count|\\n|produce_throttle_time_avg |  | int | ms|\\n|produce_throttle_time_max |  | int | ms|\\n|record_error_rate |  | float | -|\\n|record_error_total |  | int | -|\\n|record_retry_rate |  | float | -|\\n|record_retry_total |  | int | -|\\n|record_send_rate |  | float | -|\\n|record_send_total |  | int | count|\\n|request_rate |  | float | -|\\n|request_size_avg |  | float | -|\\n|request_size_max |  | int | -|\\n|request_total |  | int | count|\\n|requests_in_flight |  | int | count|\\n|response_rate |  | float | -|\\n|response_total |  | int | count|\\n|select_rate |  | float | -|\\n|select_total |  | int | count|\\n|start_time_ms |  | int | msec|\\n|successful_authentication_no_reauth_total |  | int | count|\\n|successful_authentication_rate |  | float | -|\\n|successful_authentication_total |  | int | count|\\n|successful_reauthentication_rate |  | float | -|\\n|successful_reauthentication_total |  | int | -|\\n|version |  | string | -|\\n|waiting_threads |  | int | count |\\n\\n### `kafka_connect`\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| Tag               | Description            |\\n| :---------------- | ---------------------- |\\n| client_id | client id |\\n| connector | connector |\\n| jolokia_agent_url | Jolokia agent url path |\\n| task | task |\\n| type | metric type |\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n| Metric | Description | Type | Unit |\\n| :----- | ----------- | ---- | ---- |\\n|batch_size_avg |  | int | -|\\n|batch_size_max |  | int | -|\\n|commit_id |  | string | -|\\n|connector_class |  | string | -|\\n|connector_count |  | int | count|\\n|connector_destroyed_task_count |  | int | count|\\n|connector_failed_task_count |  | int | count|\\n|connector_paused_task_count |  | int | count|\\n|connector_restarting_task_count |  | int | count|\\n|connector_running_task_count |  | int | count|\\n|connector_startup_attempts_total |  | int | count|\\n|connector_startup_failure_percentage |  | float | -|\\n|connector_startup_failure_total |  | int | count|\\n|connector_startup_success_percentage |  | float | -|\\n|connector_startup_success_total |  | int | count|\\n|connector_total_task_count |  | int | count|\\n|connector_type |  | string | -|\\n|connector_unassigned_task_count |  | int | count|\\n|connector_version |  | string | -|\\n|count |  | int | count|\\n|deadletterqueue_produce_failures |  | int | count|\\n|deadletterqueue_produce_requests |  | int | count|\\n|last_error_timestamp | The epoch timestamp when this task last encountered an error in millisecond. | int | msec|\\n|offset_commit_avg_time_ms |  | float | ms|\\n|offset_commit_completion_rate |  | float | -|\\n|offset_commit_completion_total |  | int | count|\\n|offset_commit_failure_percentage |  | float | -|\\n|offset_commit_max_time_ms |  | float | ms|\\n|offset_commit_seq_no |  | int | -|\\n|offset_commit_skip_rate |  | float | -|\\n|offset_commit_skip_total |  | int | count|\\n|offset_commit_success_percentage |  | float | -|\\n|partition_count |  | int | count|\\n|pause_ratio |  | float | -|\\n|put_batch_avg_time_ms |  | float | ms|\\n|put_batch_max_time_ms |  | float | ms|\\n|running_ratio |  | float | -|\\n|sink_record_active_count |  | int | count|\\n|sink_record_active_count_avg |  | int | count|\\n|sink_record_active_count_max |  | int | count|\\n|sink_record_read_rate |  | float | -|\\n|sink_record_read_total |  | int | count|\\n|sink_record_send_rate |  | float | -|\\n|sink_record_send_total |  | int | count|\\n|source_record_active_count |  | int | count|\\n|source_record_poll_rate |  | float | -|\\n|source_record_poll_total |  | int | count|\\n|source_record_write_rate |  | float | -|\\n|source_record_write_total |  | int | count|\\n|start_time_ms |  | int | msec|\\n|status |  | string | -|\\n|task_count |  | int | count|\\n|task_startup_attempts_total |  | int | count|\\n|task_startup_failure_percentage |  | float | -|\\n|task_startup_failure_total |  | int | count|\\n|task_startup_success_percentage |  | float | -|\\n|task_startup_success_total |  | int | count|\\n|total_errors_logged |  | int | count|\\n|total_record_errors |  | int | count|\\n|total_record_failures |  | int | count|\\n|total_records_skipped |  | int | count|\\n|total_retries |  | int | count|\\n|version |  | string | - |"}');
INSERT INTO integrate (id,name,`key`,cate,`path`, display) VALUES (15,'Nacos','nacos',2,'prom/prom-nacos.conf',"{\"instruction\": \"# Nacos\\nNacos 性能指标展示，包括 Nacos 在线时长、Nacos Config 长链接数、Nacos Config 配置个数、Service Count、HTTP 请求次数等。\\n## 安装配置\\n### 版本支持\\n\\n- 操作系统支持：Linux / Windows\\n- Nacos 版本：>= 0.8.0\\n\\n说明：示例 Nacos 版本为 1.4.1。\\n( Linux / Windows 环境相同)\\n\\n### 指标采集 (必选)\\n\\n- 配置 application.properties 文件，暴露 metrics 数据\\n\\n```\\nmanagement.endpoints.web.exposure.include=*\\n```\\n\\n- 重启 Nacos\\n\\n集群方式和单例模式启动参数有差异，具体参考 [Nacos 官方文档](https://nacos.io/zh-cn/docs/quick-start.html)。\\n\\n- 校验\\n\\n访问 {ip}:8848/nacos/actuator/prometheus，看是否能访问到 metrics 数据\\n\\n- 开启 datasleuth Prometheus 插件\\n\\n```\\ncd /usr/local/datasleuth/conf.d/prom/\\ncp prom.conf.sample nacos-prom.conf\\n```\\n\\n- 修改 nacos-prom.conf 配置文件\\n\\n主要参数说明\\n\\n- urls：prometheus 指标地址，这里填写 Nacos 暴露出来的指标 url\\n- source：采集器别名，建议写成nacos\\n- interval：采集间隔\\n- measurement_prefix：指标前缀，便于指标分类查询\\n- tls_open：TLS 配置\\n- metric_types：指标类型，不填，代表采集所有指标\\n\\n```\\n[[inputs.prom]]\\n  urls = [\\\"http://192.168.0.189:8848/nacos/actuator/prometheus\\\"]\\n  ## 忽略对 url 的请求错误\\n  ignore_req_err = false\\n  ## 采集器别名\\n  source = \\\"nacos\\\"\\n  metric_types = []\\n  measurement_prefix = \\\"nacos_\\\"\\n  ## 采集间隔 \\\"ns\\\", \\\"us\\\" (or \\\"µs\\\"), \\\"ms\\\", \\\"s\\\", \\\"m\\\", \\\"h\\\"\\n  interval = \\\"10s\\\"\\n  ## TLS 配置\\n  tls_open = false\\n  ## 自定义Tags\\n  [inputs.prom.tags]\\n  # some_tag = \\\"some_value\\\"\\n  # more_tag = \\\"some_other_value\\\"\\n```\\n\",\n  \"metrics\": \"## 指标详解\\n### JVM metrics\\n| **指标** | **含义** |\\n| --- | --- |\\n| system_cpu_usage | CPU使用率 |\\n| system_load_average_1m | load |\\n| jvm_memory_used_bytes | 内存使用字节，包含各种内存区 |\\n| jvm_memory_max_bytes | 内存最大字节，包含各种内存区 |\\n| jvm_gc_pause_seconds_count | gc次数，包含各种gc |\\n| jvm_gc_pause_seconds_sum | gc耗时，包含各种gc |\\n| jvm_threads_daemon | 线程数 |\\n\\n### Nacos 监控指标\\n| **指标** | **含义** |\\n| --- | --- |\\n| http_server_requests_seconds_count | http请求次数，包括多种(url,方法,code) |\\n| http_server_requests_seconds_sum | http请求总耗时，包括多种(url,方法,code) |\\n| nacos_timer_seconds_sum | Nacos config 水平通知耗时 |\\n| nacos_timer_seconds_count | Nacos config 水平通知次数 |\\n| nacos_monitor{name='longPolling'} | Nacos config长连接数 |\\n| nacos_monitor{name='configCount'} | Nacos config配置个数 |\\n| nacos_monitor{name='dumpTask'} | Nacos config配置落盘任务堆积数 |\\n| nacos_monitor{name='notifyTask'} | Nacos config配置水平通知任务堆积数 |\\n| nacos_monitor{name='getConfig'} | Nacos config读配置统计数 |\\n| nacos_monitor{name='publish'} | Nacos config写配置统计数 |\\n| nacos_monitor{name='ipCount'} | Nacos naming ip个数 |\\n| nacos_monitor{name='domCount'} | Nacos naming域名个数(1.x 版本) |\\n| nacos_monitor{name='serviceCount'} | Nacos naming域名个数(2.x 版本) |\\n| nacos_monitor{name='failedPush'} | Nacos naming推送失败数 |\\n| nacos_monitor{name='avgPushCost'} | Nacos naming平均推送耗时 |\\n| nacos_monitor{name='leaderStatus'} | Nacos naming角色状态 |\\n| nacos_monitor{name='maxPushCost'} | Nacos naming最大推送耗时 |\\n| nacos_monitor{name='mysqlhealthCheck'} | Nacos naming mysql健康检查次数 |\\n| nacos_monitor{name='httpHealthCheck'} | Nacos naming http健康检查次数 |\\n| nacos_monitor{name='tcpHealthCheck'} | Nacos naming tcp健康检查次数 |\\n\\n### Nacos 异常指标\\n| **指标** | **含义** |\\n| --- | --- |\\n| nacos_exception_total{name='db'} | 数据库异常 |\\n| nacos_exception_total{name='configNotify'} | Nacos config水平通知失败 |\\n| nacos_exception_total{name='unhealth'} | Nacos config server之间健康检查异常 |\\n| nacos_exception_total{name='disk'} | Nacos naming写磁盘异常 |\\n| nacos_exception_total{name='leaderSendBeatFailed'} | Nacos naming leader发送心跳异常 |\\n| nacos_exception_total{name='illegalArgument'} | 请求参数不合法 |\\n| nacos_exception_total{name='nacos'} | Nacos请求响应内部错误异常（读写失败，没权限，参数错误） |\\n\\n更多 Nacos 指标，可以参考 [Nacos 官方网站-监控](https://nacos.io/zh-cn/docs/monitor-guide.html)\\n\\\"}\"}\n"
);
INSERT INTO integrate (id,name,`key`,cate,`path`, display) VALUES
	 (16,'DM8','dm8',1,'prom/prom-dm.conf',"{\"instruction\": \"# 达梦数据库（DM8）\\n## 安装配置\\n### 前提条件\\n\\n- 安装 DM8\\n- 安装 DEM\\n\\n**DEM** 全称为 Dameng Enterprise Manager，是达梦提供的、基于 java 编写的、用于监控达梦数据库的 web 工具。\\n目录为$DM_HOME/web，当前目录存放 DEM 安装手册(DEM.pdf)和运行程序dem.war，按照手册进行部署即可。\\n### 指标暴露\\nDEM 默认暴露指标端口为：8090，可通过浏览器查看指标相关信息：http://clientIP:8090/dem/metrics。如果访问不通，则说明没有开通指标暴露能力，可以按照以下步骤操作。\\n\\n- 添加数据库\\n\\n默认情况下，DEM 安装成功后并不直接监控数据库，需要进行配置后才数据库才会被监控。\\n\\n1. 点击菜单 **智能运维** -> **资源监控**，底部切换 Tab 为**数据库**，\\n2. 点击添加**单实例/集群**，填写被监控的数据库信息。\\n3. 配置 Prometheus\\n4. 点击菜单 **系统管理** -> **系统设置**，在**其他功能**模块选择prometheus_metric_nodes，\\n5. 勾选需要被监控的数据库。点击**确定**按钮，完成配置。\\n6. 验证\\n\\n通过浏览器查看指标相关信息：http://clientIP:8090/dem/metrics。端口为 DEM 访问端口，可能有差异，以实际端口为准即可。\\n### datasleuth 采集器配置\\n由于DEM能够直接暴露metrics url，所以可以直接通过prom采集器进行采集。\\n调整内容如下：\\n\\n```\\nurls = [\\\"http://clientIP:8090/dem/metrics\\\"]\\nsource = \\\"dm\\\"\\ninterval = \\\"10s\\\"\\n```\\n_其他配置按需调整_\\n，调整参数说明 ：\\n\\n- urls：prometheus指标地址，这里填写对应组件暴露出来的指标 url\\n- source：采集器别名，建议做区分\\n- interval：采集间隔\\n### 重启 datasleuth\\nsystemctl restart datasleuth\\n\",\n  \"metrics\": \"## 指标\\n| **指标** | **描述** |\\n| --- | --- |\\n| global_status_sessions | 会话数 |\\n| global_status_threads | 线程 |\\n| global_status_tps | TPS |\\n| global_status_qps | QPS |\\n| global_status_ddlps | ddlps |\\n| global_status_ips | 每秒 instert 数 |\\n| global_status_ups | 每秒 update 数 |\\n| global_status_dps | 每秒 delete 数 |\\n| mf_status_memory_mem_used_bytes | 已使用内存大小 |\\n| mf_status_memory_mem_total_bytes | 总内存大小 |\\n| mf_status_disk_used_bytes | 已使用磁盘大小 |\\n| mf_status_disk_total_bytes | 总共磁盘大小 |\\n| mf_status_disk_read_speed_bytes | 磁盘读取速率 |\\n| mf_status_disk_write_speed_bytes | 写入速率 |\\n| mf_status_network_receive_speed_bytes | 接收速率 |\\n| mf_status_network_transmit_speed_bytes | 发送速率 |\"}\n"
);
INSERT INTO integrate (id,name,`key`,cate,`path`, display) VALUES(17,'Flink','flink',2,'flinkv1/flinkv1.conf',
"{\"instruction\": \"# Flink\\nFlink 采集器可以从 Flink 实例中采取很多指标，比如 Flink 服务器状态和网络的状态等多种指标，并将指标采集到观测云 ，帮助你监控分析 Flink 各种异常情况。\\n## 配置\\n说明：示例 Flink 版本为 Flink 1.14.2 (CentOS)，各个不同版本指标可能存在差异。\\n目前 Flink 官方提供两种 metrics 上报方式：[Prometheus](https://nightlies.apache.org/flink/flink-docs-release-1.14/docs/deployment/metric_reporters/#prometheus) 和 [Prometheus PushGateway](https://nightlies.apache.org/flink/flink-docs-release-1.14/docs/deployment/metric_reporters/#prometheuspushgateway)。它们主要的区别是：\\n\\n- Prometheus PushGateway 方式是把集群所有的 metrics 统一汇报给 PushGateway，所以需要额外安装 PushGateway。\\n- Prometheus 方式需要集群每个节点暴露一个唯一端口，不需要额外安装其它软件，但需要 N 个可用端口，配置略微复杂。\\n### PrometheusPushGateway 方式（推荐）\\n\\n- 下载和安装：PushGateWay 可以在 [Prometheus 官方页面](https://prometheus.io/download/#pushgateway) 进行下载。\\n\\n启动 Push Gateway：（此命令仅供参考，具体命令根据实际环境可能有所不同）\\n\\n```\\nnohup ./pushgateway &\\n```\\n\\n- 配置 flink-conf.yaml 把 metrics 统一汇报给 PushGateway\\n\\n配置 Flink 的配置文件 conf/flink-conf.yaml 示例：\\n\\n```\\nmetrics.reporter.promgateway.class: org.apache.flink.metrics.prometheus.PrometheusPushGatewayReporter # 固定这个值，不能改\\nmetrics.reporter.promgateway.host: localhost # promgateway 的 IP 地址\\nmetrics.reporter.promgateway.port: 9091 # promgateway 的监听端口\\nmetrics.reporter.promgateway.interval: 15 SECONDS # 采集间隔\\nmetrics.reporter.promgateway.groupingKey: k1=v1;k2=v2\\n\\n# 以下是可选参数\\n# metrics.reporter.promgateway.jobName: myJob\\n# metrics.reporter.promgateway.randomJobNameSuffix: true\\n# metrics.reporter.promgateway.deleteOnShutdown: false\\n```\\n启动 Flink：./bin/start-cluster.sh（此命令仅供参考，具体命令根据实际环境可能有所不同）\\n### Prometheus 方式\\n\\n- 配置 flink-conf.yaml 暴露各个节点的 metrics。配置 Flink 的配置文件 conf/flink-conf.yaml 示例：\\n\\n```\\nmetrics.reporter.prom.class: org.apache.flink.metrics.prometheus.PrometheusReporter\\nmetrics.reporter.prom.port: 9250-9260 # 各个节点的端口区间，根据节点数量有所不同，一个端口对应一个节点\\n```\\n\\n- 启动 Flink: ./bin/start-cluster.sh（此命令仅供参考，具体命令根据实际环境可能有所不同）\\n- 更改 Flink 配置添加如下内容，开启 Prometheus 采集\\n\\n```\\nmetrics.reporter.prom.class: org.apache.flink.metrics.prometheus.PrometheusReporter\\nmetrics.reporter.prom.port: 9250-9260\\n```\\n注意：metrics.reporter.prom.port 设置请参考集群 jobmanager 和 taskmanager 数量而定\\n\\n- 重启 Flink 集群应用配置\\n- curl http://{Flink iP}:9250-9260 返回结果正常即可开始采集\\n\",\n  \"metrics\": \"## 指标\\n默认情况下，Flink 会收集多个指标，这些[指标](https://nightlies.apache.org/flink/flink-docs-release-1.14/docs/ops/metrics/#system-metrics)可提供对当前状态的深入洞察。\\n### flink_jobmanager\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| **Tag** | **Description** |\\n| --- | --- |\\n| host | Host name. |\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| **Metric** | **Description** | **Type** | **Unit** |\\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\\n| Status_JVM_CPU_Load | The recent CPU usage of the JVM. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_CPU_Time | The CPU time used by the JVM. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_ClassLoader_ClassesLoaded | The total number of classes loaded since the start of the JVM. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_ClassLoader_ClassesUnloaded | The total number of classes unloaded since the start of the JVM. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_GarbageCollector_Copy_Count | The total number of collections that have occurred. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_GarbageCollector_Copy_Time | The total time spent performing garbage collection. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_GarbageCollector_G1_Old_Generation_Count | The total number of collections that have occurred. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_GarbageCollector_G1_Old_Generation_Time | The total time spent performing garbage collection. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_GarbageCollector_G1_Young_Generation_Count | The total number of collections that have occurred. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_GarbageCollector_G1_Young_Generation_Time | The total time spent performing garbage collection. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_GarbageCollector_MarkSweepCompact_Count | The total number of collections that have occurred. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_GarbageCollector_MarkSweepCompact_Time | The total time spent performing garbage collection. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_Direct_Count | The number of buffers in the direct buffer pool. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_Direct_MemoryUsed | The amount of memory used by the JVM for the direct buffer pool. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_Direct_TotalCapacity | The total capacity of all buffers in the direct buffer pool. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_Heap_Committed | The amount of heap memory guaranteed to be available to the JVM. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_Heap_Max | The maximum amount of heap memory that can be used for memory management. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_Heap_Used | The amount of heap memory currently used. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_Mapped_Count | The number of buffers in the mapped buffer pool. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_Mapped_MemoryUsed | The amount of memory used by the JVM for the mapped buffer pool. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_Mapped_TotalCapacity | The number of buffers in the mapped buffer pool. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_Metaspace_Committed | The amount of memory guaranteed to be available to the JVM in the meta-space memory pool (in bytes). | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_Metaspace_Max | The maximum amount of memory that can be used in the meta-space memory pool (in bytes). | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_Metaspace_Used | Used bytes of a given JVM memory area | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_NonHeap_Committed | The amount of non-heap memory guaranteed to be available to the JVM. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_NonHeap_Max | The maximum amount of non-heap memory that can be used for memory management | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_NonHeap_Used | The amount of non-heap memory currently used. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Threads_Count | The total number of live threads. | int | count |\\n| numRegisteredTaskManagers | The number of registered task managers. | int | count |\\n| numRunningJobs | The number of running jobs. | int | count |\\n| taskSlotsAvailable | The number of available task slots. | int | count |\\n| taskSlotsTotal | The total number of task slots. | int | count |\\n\\n### flink_taskmanager\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| **Tag** | **Description** |\\n| --- | --- |\\n| host | Host name. |\\n| tm_id | Task manager ID. |\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| **Metric** | **Description** | **Type** | **Unit** |\\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\\n| Status_Flink_Memory_Managed_Total | The total amount of managed memory. | int | count |\\n| Status_Flink_Memory_Managed_Used | The amount of managed memory currently used. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_CPU_Load | The recent CPU usage of the JVM. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_CPU_Time | The CPU time used by the JVM. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_ClassLoader_ClassesLoaded | The total number of classes loaded since the start of the JVM. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_ClassLoader_ClassesUnloaded | The total number of classes unloaded since the start of the JVM. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_GarbageCollector_G1_Old_Generation_Count | The total number of collections that have occurred. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_GarbageCollector_G1_Old_Generation_Time | The total time spent performing garbage collection. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_GarbageCollector_G1_Young_Generation_Count | The total number of collections that have occurred. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_GarbageCollector_G1_Young_Generation_Time | The total time spent performing garbage collection. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_Direct_Count | The number of buffers in the direct buffer pool. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_Direct_MemoryUsed | The amount of memory used by the JVM for the direct buffer pool. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_Direct_TotalCapacity | The total capacity of all buffers in the direct buffer pool. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_Heap_Committed | The amount of heap memory guaranteed to be available to the JVM. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_Heap_Max | The maximum amount of heap memory that can be used for memory management. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_Heap_Used | The amount of heap memory currently used. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_Mapped_Count | The number of buffers in the mapped buffer pool. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_Mapped_MemoryUsed | The amount of memory used by the JVM for the mapped buffer pool. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_Mapped_TotalCapacity | The number of buffers in the mapped buffer pool. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_Metaspace_Committed | The amount of memory guaranteed to be available to the JVM in the meta-space memory pool (in bytes). | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_Metaspace_Max | The maximum amount of memory that can be used in the meta-space memory pool (in bytes). | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_Metaspace_Used | Used bytes of a given JVM memory area | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_NonHeap_Committed | The amount of non-heap memory guaranteed to be available to the JVM. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_NonHeap_Max | The maximum amount of non-heap memory that can be used for memory management | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Memory_NonHeap_Used | The amount of non-heap memory currently used. | int | count |\\n| Status_JVM_Threads_Count | The total number of live threads. | int | count |\\n| Status_Network_AvailableMemorySegments | The number of unused memory segments. | int | count |\\n| Status_Network_TotalMemorySegments | The number of allocated memory segments. | int | count |\\n| Status_Shuffle_Netty_AvailableMemory | The amount of unused memory in bytes. | int | count |\\n| Status_Shuffle_Netty_AvailableMemorySegments | The number of unused memory segments. | int | count |\\n| Status_Shuffle_Netty_RequestedMemoryUsage | Experimental: The usage of the network memory. Shows (as percentage) the total amount of requested memory from all of the subtasks. It can exceed 100% as not all requested memory is required for subtask to make progress. However if usage exceeds 100% throughput can suffer greatly and please consider increasing available network memory, or decreasing configured size of network buffer pools. | int | count |\\n| Status_Shuffle_Netty_TotalMemory | The amount of allocated memory in bytes. | int | count |\\n| Status_Shuffle_Netty_TotalMemorySegments | The number of allocated memory segments. | int | count |\\n| Status_Shuffle_Netty_UsedMemory | The amount of used memory in bytes. | int | count |\\n| Status_Shuffle_Netty_UsedMemorySegments | The number of used memory segments. | int | count |\\n\\n\"}\n"
);
INSERT INTO integrate (id,name,`key`,cate,`path`, display) VALUES (18,'Memcached','memcached',1,'db/memcached.conf',
"{\"instruction\": \"# Memcached\\n\\nMemcached 采集器可以从 Memcached 实例中采集实例运行状态指标，并将指标采集到观测云，帮助监控分析 Memcached 各种异常情况。\\n## 配置\\n### 前置条件\\n\\n- Memcached 版本 >= 1.5.0。已测试的版本：\\n- 1.5.x\\n- 1.6.x\\n配置memcached.conf。示例如下：\\n```\\n[[inputs.memcached]]\\n  ## Servers' addresses.\\n  servers = [\\\"localhost:11211\\\"]\\n  # unix_sockets = [\\\"/var/run/memcached.sock\\\"]\\n\\n  ## Set true to enable election\\n  election = true\\n\\n  ## Collect extra stats\\n  # extra_stats = [\\\"slabs\\\", \\\"items\\\"]\\n\\n  ## Collect interval.\\n  # 单位 \\\"ns\\\", \\\"us\\\" (or \\\"µs\\\"), \\\"ms\\\", \\\"s\\\", \\\"m\\\", \\\"h\\\"\\n  interval = \\\"10s\\\"\\n\\n[inputs.memcached.tags]\\n  # some_tag = \\\"some_value\\\"\\n  # more_tag = \\\"some_other_value\\\"\\n  # ...\\n```\\n\\n\",\n  \"metrics\": \"## 指标\\n以下所有数据采集，默认会追加名为 host 的全局 tag（tag 值为 datasleuth 所在主机名），也可以在配置中通过 [inputs.memcached.tags] 指定其它标签：\\n\\n```\\n[inputs.memcached.tags]\\n  # some_tag = \\\"some_value\\\"\\n  # more_tag = \\\"some_other_value\\\"\\n  # ...\\n```\\n### memcached\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| **Tag** | **Description** |\\n| --- | --- |\\n| server | The host name from which metrics are gathered |\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| **Metric** | **Description** | **Type** | **Unit** |\\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\\n| accepting_conns | Whether or not server is accepting conns | int | count |\\n| auth_cmds | Number of authentication commands handled, success or failure | int | count |\\n| auth_errors | Number of failed authentications | int | count |\\n| bytes | Current number of bytes used to store items | int | B |\\n| bytes_read | Total number of bytes read by this server from network | int | B |\\n| bytes_written | Total number of bytes sent by this server to network | int | B |\\n| cas_badval | Number of CAS for which a key was found, but the CAS value did not match | int | count |\\n| cas_hits | Number of successful CAS requests | int | count |\\n| cas_misses | Number of CAS requests against missing keys | int | count |\\n| cmd_flush | Cumulative number of flush requests | int | count |\\n| cmd_get | Cumulative number of retrieval requests | int | count |\\n| cmd_set | Cumulative number of storage requests | int | count |\\n| cmd_touch | Cumulative number of touch requests | int | count |\\n| conn_yields | Number of times any connection yielded to another due to hitting the -R limit | int | count |\\n| connection_structures | Number of connection structures allocated by the server | int | count |\\n| curr_connections | Number of open connections | int | count |\\n| curr_items | Current number of items stored | int | count |\\n| decr_hits | Number of successful decr requests | int | count |\\n| decr_misses | Number of decr requests against missing keys | int | count |\\n| delete_hits | Number of deletion requests resulting in an item being removed | int | count |\\n| delete_misses | umber of deletions requests for missing keys | int | count |\\n| evicted_unfetched | Items evicted from LRU that were never touched by get/incr/append/etc | int | count |\\n| evictions | Number of valid items removed from cache to free memory for new items | int | count |\\n| expired_unfetched | Items pulled from LRU that were never touched by get/incr/append/etc before expiring | int | count |\\n| get_hits | Number of keys that have been requested and found present | int | count |\\n| get_misses | Number of items that have been requested and not found | int | count |\\n| hash_bytes | Bytes currently used by hash tables | int | B |\\n| hash_is_expanding | Indicates if the hash table is being grown to a new size | int | count |\\n| hash_power_level | Current size multiplier for hash table | int | count |\\n| incr_hits | Number of successful incr requests | int | count |\\n| incr_misses | Number of incr requests against missing keys | int | count |\\n| limit_maxbytes | Number of bytes this server is allowed to use for storage | int | B |\\n| listen_disabled_num | Number of times server has stopped accepting new connections (maxconns) | int | count |\\n| reclaimed | Number of times an entry was stored using memory from an expired entry | int | count |\\n| threads | Number of worker threads requested | int | count |\\n| total_connections | Total number of connections opened since the server started running | int | count |\\n| total_items | Total number of items stored since the server started | int | count |\\n| touch_hits | Number of keys that have been touched with a new expiration time | int | count |\\n| touch_misses | Number of items that have been touched and not found | int | count |\\n| uptime | Number of secs since the server started | int | count |\\n\\n### memcached_items\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| **Tag** | **Description** |\\n| --- | --- |\\n| server | The host name from which metrics are gathered |\\n| slab_id | The id of the current slab |\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| **Metric** | **Description** | **Type** | **Unit** |\\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\\n| age | Age of the oldest item in the LRU | int | count |\\n| crawler_reclaimed | Number of items which freed by the LRU Crawler | int | count |\\n| direct_reclaims | Number of worker threads which had to directly pull LRU tails to find memory for a new item | int | count |\\n| evicted | Number of the items which had to be evicted from the LRU before expiring | int | count |\\n| evicted_nonzero | Number of the onzero items which had an explicit expire time set had to be evicted from the LRU before expiring | int | count |\\n| evicted_time | Seconds since the last access for the most recent item evicted from this class | int | s |\\n| evicted_unfetched | Number of the valid items evicted from the LRU which were never touched after being set | int | count |\\n| expired_unfetched | Number of the expired items reclaimed from the LRU which were never touched after being set | int | count |\\n| lrutail_reflocked | Number of items which found to be refcount locked in the LRU tail | int | count |\\n| moves_to_cold | Number of items which were moved from HOT or WARM into COLD | int | count |\\n| moves_to_warm | Number of items which were moved from COLD to WARM | int | count |\\n| moves_within_lru | Number of active items which were bumped within HOT or WARM | int | count |\\n| number | Number of items presently stored in this slab class | int | count |\\n| number_cold | Number of items presently stored in the COLD LRU | int | count |\\n| number_hot | Number of items presently stored in the HOT LRU | int | count |\\n| number_noexp | Number of items presently stored in the NOEXP class | int | count |\\n| number_warm | Number of items presently stored in the WARM LRU | int | count |\\n| outofmemory | Number of the underlying slab class which was unable to store a new item | int | count |\\n| reclaimed | Number of entries which were stored using memory from an expired entry | int | count |\\n| tailrepairs | How many times memcache self-healed a slab with a refcount leak | int | count |\\n\\n### memcached_slabs\\n\\n- 标签\\n\\n| **Tag** | **Description** |\\n| --- | --- |\\n| server | The host name from which metrics are gathered |\\n| slab_id | The id of the current slab |\\n\\n- 指标列表\\n\\n| **Metric** | **Description** | **Type** | **Unit** |\\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\\n| active_slabs | Total number of slab classes allocated | int | count |\\n| cas_badval | Number of CAS commands failed to modify a value due to a bad CAS id | int | count |\\n| cas_hits | Number of CAS commands modified this slab class | int | count |\\n| chunk_size | The amount of space each chunk uses | int | B |\\n| chunks_per_page | How many chunks exist within one page | int | count |\\n| cmd_set | Number of set requests stored data in this slab class | int | count |\\n| decr_hits | Number of decrs commands modified this slab class | int | count |\\n| delete_hits | Number of delete commands succeeded in this slab class | int | count |\\n| free_chunks | Chunks not yet allocated to items or freed via delete | int | count |\\n| free_chunks_end | Number of free chunks at the end of the last allocated page | int | count |\\n| get_hits | Number of get requests were serviced by this slab class | int | count |\\n| incr_hits | Number of incrs commands modified this slab class | int | count |\\n| total_chunks | Total number of chunks allocated to the slab class | int | count |\\n| total_malloced | Total amount of memory allocated to slab pages | int | B |\\n| total_pages | Total number of pages allocated to the slab class | int | count |\\n| touch_hits | Number of touches serviced by this slab class | int | count |\\n| used_chunks | How many chunks have been allocated to items | int | count |\\n\\n\"}\n"
);
INSERT INTO integrate (id,name,`key`,cate,`path`, display) VALUES (19,'Rocketmq','rocketmq',2,'prom/prom-rocketmq.conf',
	 "{\"instruction\": \"# RocketMQ\\nRocketMQ 指标展示，包括生产者 TPS/消息大小、消费者 TPS/消息大小、消息堆积、topic 信息等\\n## 安装配置\\n说明：示例 Linux 版本为 CentOS Linux release 7.8.2003 (Core)，Windows 版本请修改对应的配置文件\\n\\n- 操作系统支持：Linux / Windows\\n- 服务器安装 rocketmq-exporter\\n### 安装 exporter\\n\\n- 拉取 rocketmq-exporter\\n\\n```\\ngit clone https://github.com/apache/rocketmq-exporter.git\\n```\\n\\n- 进入安装目录\\n\\n```\\ncd rocketmq-exporter/\\n```\\n\\n- 构建安装包 (2选1即可)\\n\\n（1）构建 jar 包方式\\n\\n```\\nmvn clean install\\n```\\n构建完成，进入 target 目录\\n\\n```\\ncd target\\n```\\n启动 jar 包 (替换命令行中 nameserverip 地址)\\n\\n```\\nnohup java -jar target/rocketmq-exporter-0.0.2-SNAPSHOT.jar --rocketmq.config.namesrvAddr=nameserverip:9876 &\\n```\\n（2）构建 Docker 镜像方式\\n\\n```\\nmvn package -Dmaven.test.skip=true docker:build\\n```\\n使用镜像启动 Docker (替换命令行中 nameserverip 地址)\\n\\n```\\ndocker run -d --net=\\\"host\\\" --name rocketmq-exporter -p 5557:5557 docker.io/rocketmq-exporter --rocketmq.config.namesrvAddr=nameserverip:9876\\n```\\n\\n- 测试 rocketmq-exporter 是否正常\\n\\n```\\ncurl http://127.0.0.1:5557/metrics\\n```\\n### 指标采集\\n\\n- 开启 datasleuth Prometheus 插件，复制 sample 文件\\n\\n```\\ncd /usr/local/datasleuth/conf.d/prom\\ncp prom.conf.sample prom.conf\\n```\\n\\n- 修改配置文件 prom.conf\\n\\n主要参数说明\\n\\n- urls：exporter 地址，建议填写内网地址，远程采集可使用公网\\n- ignore_req_err：忽略对 url 的请求错误\\n- source：采集器别名\\n- metrics_types：默认只采集 counter 和 gauge 类型的指标\\n- interval：采集频率\\n\\n```\\n[[inputs.prom]]\\n  urls = [\\\"http://127.0.0.1:5557/metrics\\\"]\\n  ignore_req_err = false\\n  source = \\\"prom\\\"\\n# metric_types 需要选择空，rocketmq-exporter 没有指定数据类型\\n  metric_types = []\\n  interval = \\\"60s\\\"\\n```\\n\",\n  \"metrics\": \"## 指标\\n| **指标** | **描述** | **数据类型** |\\n| --- | --- | --- |\\n| rocketmq_broker_tps | broker每秒生产消息数量 | int |\\n| rocketmq_broker_qps | broker每秒消费消息数量 | int |\\n| rocketmq_producer_tps | 某个topic每秒生产的消息数量 | int |\\n| rocketmq_producer_put_size | 某个topic每秒生产的消息大小(字节) | int |\\n| rocketmq_producer_offset | 某个topic的生产消息的进度 | int |\\n| rocketmq_consumer_tps | 某个消费组每秒消费的消息数量 | int |\\n| rocketmq_consumer_get_size | 某个消费组每秒消费的消息大小(字节) | int |\\n| rocketmq_consumer_offset | 某个消费组的消费消息的进度 | int |\\n| rocketmq_group_get_latency_by_storetime | 某个消费组的消费延时时间 | int |\\n| rocketmq_message_accumulati | 消息堆积量 | int |\\n\"}\n" );
INSERT INTO integrate (id,name,`key`,cate,`path`, display) VALUES (20,'Zookeeper','zookeeper',2,'prom/prom-zookeeper.conf',
	 "{\"instruction\": \"## 安装部署\\n说明：示例 ZooKeeper 版本为 3.6.3 (CentOS)， 3.6 +的版本会比之前版本多出许多指标，如果您使用的是 3.6 之前的版本可能会存在部分指标采集不到的情况。\\n### 开启 Zookeeper Metrics\\n\\n- 开启 Zookeeper 中的 Metrics Providers 配置（默认配置文件位置：Zookeeper 安装目录 /conf/zoo.cfg）并添加 4lw.commands.whitelist=*\\n\\n```\\n> ## Metrics Providers\\n> #\\n> # [https://prometheus.io](https://prometheus.io) Metrics Exporter\\n> metricsProvider.className=org.apache.zookeeper.metrics.prometheus.PrometheusMetricsProvider\\n> metricsProvider.httpPort=7000\\n> metricsProvider.exportJvmInfo=true\\n> 4lw.commands.whitelist=*\\n```\\n\\n- 重启 ZooKeeper 集群应用配置\\n- 在 ZooKeeper 集群中下载安装 [zookeeper_exporter](https://github.com/carlpett/zookeeper_exporter/releases/download/v1.1.0/zookeeper_exporter)， chmod +x 赋予执行权限后启动即可。默认端口为 9141 ,可用通过命令进行验证数据\\n\\n```\\n[root@d ~]# curl 0.0.0.0:9141/metrics\\nzk_sync_process_time{quantile=\\\"0_5\\\",zk_host=\\\"172.16.0.196:2181\\\"} NaN\\nzk_proposal_latency_sum{zk_host=\\\"172.16.0.196:2181\\\"} 0.0\\nzk_read_final_proc_time_ms{quantile=\\\"0_5\\\",zk_host=\\\"172.16.0.196:2181\\\"} NaN\\nzk_process_start_time_seconds{zk_host=\\\"172.16.0.23:2181\\\"} 1.645516624379E9\\nzk_jvm_buffer_pool_capacity_bytes{pool=\\\"direct\\\",zk_host=\\\"172.16.0.194:2181\\\"} 287560.0\\nzk_time_waiting_empty_pool_in_commit_processor_read_ms_sum{zk_host=\\\"172.16.0.194:2181\\\"} 0.0\\nzk_stale_requests_dropped{zk_host=\\\"172.16.0.23:2181\\\"} 0.0\\nzk_open_file_descriptor_count{zk_host=\\\"172.16.0.196:2181\\\"} 83.0\\nzk_commit_commit_proc_req_queued_sum{zk_host=\\\"172.16.0.194:2181\\\"} 0.0\\nzk_write_final_proc_time_ms_count{zk_host=\\\"172.16.0.194:2181\\\"} 0.0\\nzk_election_time{quantile=\\\"0_5\\\",zk_host=\\\"172.16.0.23:2181\\\"} NaN\\nzk_connection_token_deficit_count{zk_host=\\\"172.16.0.23:2181\\\"} 2.0\\n......\\n```\\n### 开启 datasleuth 采集器\\n\\n- 开启 datasleuth Prom 插件，复制 sample 文件\\n\\n```\\n/usr/local/datasleuth/conf.d/prom\\ncp prom.conf.sample prom.conf\\n```\\n\\n- 修改 prom.conf 配置文件\\n\\n**配置如下**\\n```\\n[[inputs.prom]]\\n## Exporter URLs\\nurls = [\\\"[http://127.0.0.1:9141/metrics\\\"]](http://127.0.0.1:9141/metrics\\\"])\\n\\n## 忽略对 url 的请求错误\\nignore_req_err = false\\n\\n## 采集器别名\\nsource = \\\"zookeeper\\\"\\n\\n## 采集数据输出源\\n# 配置此项，可以将采集到的数据写到本地文件而不将数据打到中心\\n# 之后可以直接用 datasleuth --prom-conf /path/to/this/conf 命令对本地保存的指标集进行调试\\n# 如果已经将 url 配置为本地文件路径，则 --prom-conf 优先调试 output 路径的数据\\n# output = \\\"/abs/path/to/file\\\"\\n> \\n## 采集数据大小上限，单位为字节\\n# 将数据输出到本地文件时，可以设置采集数据大小上限\\n# 如果采集数据的大小超过了此上限，则采集的数据将被丢弃\\n# 采集数据大小上限默认设置为32MB\\n# max_file_size = 0\\n\\n## 指标类型过滤, 可选值为 counter, gauge, histogram, summary\\n# 默认只采集 counter 和 gauge 类型的指标\\n# 如果为空，则不进行过滤\\nmetric_types = [\\\"counter\\\", \\\"gauge\\\"]\\n\\n## 指标名称过滤\\n# 支持正则，可以配置多个，即满足其中之一即可\\n# 如果为空，则不进行过滤\\n# metric_name_filter = [\\\"cpu\\\"]\\n\\n## 指标集名称前缀\\n# 配置此项，可以给指标集名称添加前缀\\nmeasurement_prefix = \\\"\\\"\\n\\n## 指标集名称\\n# 默认会将指标名称以下划线\\\"_\\\"进行切割，切割后的第一个字段作为指标集名称，剩下字段作为当前指标名称\\n# 如果配置measurement_name, 则不进行指标名称的切割\\n# 最终的指标集名称会添加上measurement_prefix前缀\\n# measurement_name = \\\"prom\\\"\\n\\n## 采集间隔 \\\"ns\\\", \\\"us\\\" (or \\\"µs\\\"), \\\"ms\\\", \\\"s\\\", \\\"m\\\", \\\"h\\\"\\ninterval = \\\"10s\\\"\\n\\n## 过滤tags, 可配置多个tag\\n# 匹配的tag将被忽略\\n# tags_ignore = [\\\"xxxx\\\"]\\n\\n## TLS 配置\\ntls_open = false\\n# tls_ca = \\\"/tmp/ca.crt\\\"\\n# tls_cert = \\\"/tmp/peer.crt\\\"\\n# tls_key = \\\"/tmp/peer.key\\\"\\n\\n## 自定义认证方式，目前仅支持 Bearer Token\\n# token 和 token_file: 仅需配置其中一项即可\\n# [inputs.prom.auth]\\n# type = \\\"bearer_token\\\"\\n# token = \\\"xxxxxxxx\\\"\\n# token_file = \\\"/tmp/token\\\"\\n\\n## 自定义指标集名称\\n# 可以将包含前缀prefix的指标归为一类指标集\\n# 自定义指标集名称配置优先measurement_name配置项\\n#[[inputs.prom.measurements]]\\n#  prefix = \\\"cpu_\\\"\\n#  name = \\\"cpu\\\"\\n\\n# [[inputs.prom.measurements]]\\n# prefix = \\\"mem_\\\"\\n# name = \\\"mem\\\"\\n\\n## 自定义Tags\\n[inputs.prom.tags]\\n  service = \\\"zookeeper\\\"\\n# more_tag = \\\"some_other_value\\\"\\n```\\n\",\n  \"metrics\": \"## 指标详解\\n| **名称** | **描述** | **指标类型** | **Availability** |\\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\\n| **zookeeper_approximate_data_size** |  | 度量 |  |\\n| **zookeeper_avg_latency** | 服务器响应客户端请求所需的时间(ms) | 度量 |  |\\n| **zookeeper_bytes_received** | 接收的字节数 | 度量 |  |\\n| **zookeeper_bytes_sent** | 发送的字节数 | 度量 |  |\\n| **zookeeper_connections** | 客户端连接的总数(连接数) | 度量 |  |\\n| **zookeeper_ephemerals_count** |  | 度量 |  |\\n| **zookeeper_instances** |  | 度量 |  |\\n| **zookeeper_latency.avg** | 服务器响应客户端请求所需的时间（ms） | 度量 |  |\\n| **zookeeper_latency.max** | 服务器响应客户端请求所需的时间（ms） | INSERT INTO integrate (id,name,`key`,cate,`path`, display) VALUES (21,'Activemq','activemq',2,'prom/prom-activemq.conf',
    "{\"instruction\": \"# Activemq\\n\\n## 配置\\n\\n### 1. 下载JMX  Exporter\\n\\nJMX Exporter 利用 Java 的 JMX 机制来读取 JVM 运行时的一些监控数据，然后将其转换为 Prometheus 所认知的 metrics 格式，以便让 Prometheus 对其进行监控采集。\\n 下载 [jmx_prometheus_javaagent](https://github.com/prometheus/jmx_exporter/releases)到/data/public/jmx_prometheus_javaagent/目录。\\n\\n### 2.创建avtivemq.yml 配置文件\\n在/data/public/jmx_prometheus_javaagent/目录下创建activemq.yml文件，文件内容如下\\n\\n```\\nwercaseOutputName: true\\nlowercaseOutputLabelNames: true\\nblacklistObjectNames:\\n  - \\\"org.apache.activemq:clientId=*,*\\\"\\nwhitelistObjectNames:\\n  - \\\"org.apache.activemq:destinationType=Queue,*\\\"\\n  - \\\"org.apache.activemq:destinationType=Topic,*\\\"\\n  - \\\"org.apache.activemq:type=Broker,brokerName=*\\\"\\n  - \\\"org.apache.activemq:type=Topic,brokerName=*\\\"\\n```\\n### 3.activemq启动配置\\nactivemq启动时，bin/env文件中， ACTIVEMQ_OPTS参数增加-javaagent:/data/public/jmx_prometheus_javaagent/jmx_prometheus_javaagent-0.20.0.jar=51616:/data/public/jmx_prometheus_javaagent/activemq.yml。\\n全值为ACTIVEMQ_OPTS=\\\"$ACTIVEMQ_OPTS_MEMORY -javaagent:/data/public/jmx_prometheus_javaagent/jmx_prometheus_javaagent-0.20.0.jar=51616:/data/public/jmx_prometheus_javaagent/activemq.yml -Djava.util.logging.config.file=logging.properties -Djava.security.auth.login.config=$ACTIVEMQ_CONF/login.config\\\"。\\n### 4.验证采集\\n指标采集接口 curl http://172.31.65.35:51616/metrics ，检查是否可以返回metric数据。\\n### 5.接入采集配置\\ndatasleuth配置文件conf.d/prom/prom-activemq.conf中，修改urls\\n```\\n[[inputs.prom]]\\n  # 是否启用该input\\n  enable_input = true\\n\\n  # Exporter URLs.\\n  urls = [\\\"http://172.31.65.35:51616/metrics\\\"]\\n\\n  # Unix Domain Socket URL. Using socket to request data when not empty.\\n  uds_path = \\\"\\\"\\n\\n  # Ignore URL request errors.\\n  ignore_req_err = false\\n\\n  ## Collector alias.\\n  source = \\\"\\\"\\n  ......\\n\\n```\\n\",\n  \"metrics\": \"## 指标\\n\\n| **名称** | **描述** | **指标类型** | **Availability** |\\n| --- | --- | --- | --- |\\n| **jvm_memory_pool_allocated_bytes_total** | 在给定JVM内存池中分配的总字节数。仅在GC之后更新，而不是连续更新。 | 度量 |  |\\n| **jvm_memory_objects_pending_finalization** | 在终结队列中等待的对象数量。 | 度量 |  |\\n| **jvm_memory_bytes_used** | 给定JVM内存区域的已使用字节数。 | 度量 |  |\\n| **jvm_memory_bytes_committed** | 给定JVM内存区域的已提交(字节)。 | 度量 |  |\\n| **jvm_memory_bytes_max** | 给定JVM内存区域的最大字节数。 | 度量 |  |\\n| **jvm_memory_bytes_init** | 给定JVM内存区域的初始字节。 | 度量 |  |\\n| **jvm_memory_pool_bytes_used** | 给定JVM内存池中已使用的字节数。 | 度量 |  |\\n| **jvm_memory_pool_bytes_committed** | 给定JVM内存池的已提交字节数。 | 度量 |  |\\n| **jvm_memory_pool_bytes_max** | 指定JVM内存池的最大字节数。 | 度量 |  |\\n| **jvm_memory_pool_bytes_init** | 给定JVM内存池的初始字节。 | 度量 |  |\\n| **jvm_memory_pool_collection_used_bytes** | 给定JVM内存池上次收集后使用的字节数。 | 度量 |  |\\n| **jvm_memory_pool_collection_committed_bytes** | 在给定JVM内存池的最后一个收集字节之后提交。 | 度量 |  |\\n| **jvm_memory_pool_collection_max_bytes** | 给定JVM内存池最后一次收集后的最大字节数。 | 度量 |  |\\n| **jvm_memory_pool_collection_init_bytes** | 给定JVM内存池的最后一次收集字节之后的初始值。 | 度量 |  |\\n| **jvm_threads_current** | 当前JVM的线程数 | 度量 |  |\\n| **jvm_threads_daemon** |  JVM的守护线程数 | 度量 |  |\\n| **jvm_threads_peak** |  JVM的峰值线程数 | 度量 |  |\\n| **jvm_threads_started_total** |  JVM的启动线程数 | 度量 |  |\\n| **jvm_threads_deadlocked** | 等待获取对象监视器或可拥有的同步器的死锁jvm线程的周期 | 度量 |  |\\n| **jvm_threads_deadlocked_monitor** | 处于死锁状态等待获取对象监视器的jvm线程周期 | 度量 |  |\\n| **jvm_threads_state** | 按状态计算的当前线程计数 | 度量 |  |\\n| **jvm_info** | 虚拟机版本信息 | 度量 |  |\\n| **org_apache_activemq_Broker_Paused** | 对消费者的分派被暂停 | 度量 |  |\\n| **org_apache_activemq_Broker_ExpiredCount** | 已过期的消息数。org.apache.activemq: name = null,类型=代理属性= ExpiredCount | 度量 |  |\\n| **org_apache_activemq_Broker_QueueSize** | 目的地上的消息数，包括已发送但未确认的消息。org.apache.activemq:name=null,type=Broker,attribute=QueueSize | 度量 |  |\\n| **org_apache_activemq_Broker_BlockedProducerWarningInterval** | org.apache.activemq:name=null,type=Broker,attribute=BlockedProducerWarningInterval | 度量 |  |\\n| **org_apache_activemq_Broker_MaxMessageSize** | 此目标上的最大消息大小org.apache.activemq:name=null,type=Broker,attribute=MaxMessageSize | 度量 |  |\\n| **org_apache_activemq_Broker_MemoryUsageByteCount** | org.apache.activemq:name=null,type=Broker,attribute=MemoryUsageByteCount | 度量 |  |\\n| **org_apache_activemq_Broker_CurrentConnectionsCount** | rg.apache.activemq:name=null,type=Broker, Attribute =CurrentConnectionsCount | 度量 |  |\\n| **org_apache_activemq_Broker_StatisticsEnabled** | 用了代理统计信息。org.apache.activemq: name = null,类型=代理属性= StatisticsEnabled | 度量 |  |\\n| **org_apache_activemq_Broker_ProducerCount** | 加到此目标的生产者数量org.apache.activemq:name=null,type=Broker,attribute=ProducerCount | 度量 |  |\\n| **org_apache_activemq_Broker_SendDuplicateFromStoreToDLQ** | 存储消息的副本发送到死信队列的配置设置。org.apache.activemq: name = null,类型=代理属性= SendDuplicateFromStoreToDLQ | 度量 |  |\\n| **org_apache_activemq_Broker_BlockedSends** | 流控制被阻塞的消息数org.apache.activemq:name=null,type=Broker,attribute= blockedsent | 度量 |  |\\n| **org_apache_activemq_Broker_MinMessageSize** | 此目标上的最小消息大小org.apache.activemq:name=null,type=Broker,attribute=MinMessageSize | 度量 |  |\\n| **org_apache_activemq_Broker_TempUsagePercentUsage** | 用的临时使用限制的百分比org.apache.activemq:name=null,type=Broker,attribute=TempUsagePercentUsage | 度量 |  |\\n| **org_apache_activemq_Broker_CursorFull** | 消息客户端已达到消息页面的内存限制org.apache.activemq:name=null,type=Broker,attribute=CursorFull | 度量 |  |\\n| **org_apache_activemq_Broker_MaxPageSize** | rg.apache.activemq中要分页的最大消息数:name=null,type=Broker,attribute=MaxPageSize | 度量 |  |\\n| **org_apache_activemq_Broker_StorePercentUsage** | 用存储限制的百分比。org.apache.activemq: name = null,类型=代理属性= StorePercentUsage | 度量 |  |\\n| **org_apache_activemq_Broker_MaxEnqueueTime** | 消息在此目的地保存的最长时间org.apache.activemq:name=null,type=Broker,attribute=MaxEnqueueTime | 度量 |  |\\n| **org_apache_activemq_Broker_TempUsageLimit** | 分配给此目标的临时使用限制(以字节为单位)。org.apache.activemq: name = null,类型=代理属性= TempUsageLimit | 度量 |  |\\n| **org_apache_activemq_Broker_StoreLimit** | 磁盘限制，以字节为单位，用于阻塞生产者之前的持久消息。org.apache.activemq: name = null,类型=代理属性= StoreLimit | 度量 |  |\\n| **org_apache_activemq_Broker_MemoryLimit** | 传递的消息在分页到临时存储器之前使用的内存限制(以字节为单位)。org.apache.activemq: name = null,类型=代理属性= MemoryLimit | 度量 |  |\\n| **org_apache_activemq_Broker_DLQ** | 信队列org.apache.activemq:name=null,type=Broker,attribute=DLQ | 度量 |  |\\n| **org_apache_activemq_Broker_TempPercentUsage** | **org_apache_activemq_Broker_AlwaysRetroactive** | rg.apache.activemq:name=null,type=Broker,attribute=AlwaysRetroactive | 度量 |  |\\n| **org_apache_activemq_Broker_TotalMessageCount** | &\\n4、查询指标值：curl http://127.0.0.1:9165/metrics\\n5、datasleuth增加conf.d/prom/prom-keepalived.conf，修改指标采集地址\\n\\n```\\n[[inputs.prom]]\\n  # 是否启用该input\\n  enable_input = true\\n\\n  # Exporter URLs.\\n  urls = [\\\"http://172.31.65.35:9165/metrics\\\"]\\n\\n ......\\n```\\n\",\n  \"metrics\": \"## 指标\\n\\n| Metric                                          | 说明                             |\\n| ```

3. 停止n9e进程

4. 将服务器上原有n9e文件备份, config.toml备份，integrations目录备份

5. 修改config.toml文件 

   将NoSignIn 改为 false；以下配置如果有就修改，没有就添加一个：

   ```
   [HTTP.ProxyAuth]
   # if proxy auth enabled, jwt auth is disabled
   Enable = true
   TokenCheckURL = "http://172.0.0.1:9004"
   DefaultRoles = ["Admin"]
   ```

6. 将部署包中的n9e.zip解压，根据cpu架构选择amd64和arm64目录下的n9e文件，将n9e文件复制到原n9e目录下；将integrations目录拷贝到原n9e目录下。

6. 执行 sh start.sh 启动服务  

### 验证

1. 查看表integrate数据时候插入完成
2. 查看服务是否正常启动

```bash
ps -ef | grep n9e
```

可以查看到相关进程，说明部署成功

### 失败回滚

1、将备份的表integrate还原。

2、检查服务进程，关闭后删除升级包，并还原备份包，再启动

## nodeman服务升级

### 升级说明

1. 将服务器上原有的nodeman-service 下的st_nodeman 备份

2. 停止st_nodeman 进程

```shell
pid=$(ps aux | grep nodeman | grep -v grep | awk '{print $2}')
 if [ -z "$pid" ]; then
   echo "未找到进程 $proc_name"
 else
   kill $pid
   echo "成功杀死进程 $proc_name (pid: $pid)"
 fi
```

3. 修改配置文件

   ```
   新增 "auth_url": "http://172.0.0.1:9004",
   修改 "skip_auth": false,
   ```

4. 将部署包中的nodeman_service.zip解压，根据cpu架构选择amd64或者arm64中st_nodeman 文件，替换原nodeman_service目录下的st_nodeman 文件。

5. 将服务重新启动

```shell
nohup ./st_nodeman > nodeman.log 2>&1 &
```

### 验证

1. nodeman服务正常启动

```bash
ps -ef | grep st_nodeman 
```

可以查看到相关进程，说明部署成功

### 失败回滚

1、检查服务进程，关闭后删除升级包，并还原备份包和配置，再启动
